679	## chore: open Subcommand trait sweep (0.50.0-0)
680	
681	Commits: [[13]]
682	
683	Multi-step. The 12 subcommand match arms in `main.rs` repeat
684	the same `Context::load` + `try_from(&args)` + `run_command`
685	boilerplate — ~12 lines per arm. This cycle introduces a
686	`Subcommand` trait so each arm collapses to a single
687	`dispatch::<T>(args, cli.log)` line while keeping the
688	`Commands` enum as the dispatch source of truth (compile-time
689	exhaustiveness preserved). Plan-only opener; the trait + first
690	worked-example port land in `-1`.
691	
692	### Trait shape (initial sketch)
693	
694	- `Subcommand` trait on each subcommand's `Args` type:
695	  - `type Params`
696	  - `fn into_params(&self) -> Result<Self::Params, String>`
697	    — total `From` impls return `Ok(…)`; fallible `TryFrom`
698	    impls forward their error.
699	  - `fn run(ctx: &Context, params: &Self::Params)
700	    -> Result<(), Box<dyn Error>>`
701	  - `fn suppress_banner(&self) -> bool { false }` — `chid` /
702	    `desc` / `list` / `show` override.
703	  - `fn is_detached_exec(&self) -> bool { false }` —
704	    `finalize` overrides.
705	- Free helper `dispatch<S: Subcommand>(args: &S, log: …)
706	  -> ExitCode` wraps the per-arm body.
707	- `main.rs` match arms collapse to one line each; the
708	  banner / detached-exec peek logic moves behind the trait.
709	
710	Shape is subject to refinement once `-1` exercises it on
711	`chid`.
712	
713	### Ladder
714	
715	- 0.50.0-0 plan + version bump + this section + todo ladder
716	  + linkme/inventory todos (current)
717	- 0.50.0-1 add `subcommand.rs` (trait) + port `chid`
718	  (worked example)
719	- 0.50.0-2..N port remaining 11 subcommands. Grouping
720	  decided per substep; candidate split:
721	  - `From` (total): `validate_desc`, `fix_desc`, `clone`,
722	    `init`, `symlink`, `sync`, `push`
723	  - `TryFrom` (fallible): `desc`, `list`, `show`, `finalize`
724	- 0.50.0-K `main.rs` dispatch rework — drop the per-arm
725	  match boilerplate; move banner / detached-exec peeks
726	  behind the trait.
727	- 0.50.0 close-out — drop suffix, todo→Done.
728	
729	### Per-step evaluation
730	
731	Effectiveness of the trait approach is evaluated after each
732	substep. Possible outcomes at any step boundary:
733	
734	- Continue as planned.
735	- Significantly modify the trait shape (e.g. swap
736	  `into_params`/`run` for a single `dispatch` method on the
737	  trait; absorb the `Args` type as an associated type instead
738	  of `impl Subcommand for ArgsType`; etc.) — recorded in a
739	  new chores subsection at the next step.
740	- Abandon: revert the cycle to a non-trait baseline (close
741	  the cycle as a no-op with a chores `### Outcome` note
742	  capturing what didn't fit). The version-bump and todo
743	  entries land in Done as the record of "tried, didn't
744	  ship."
745	
746	This is part of the cycle's contract, not an exceptional
747	exit path — the trait sweep is the lower-risk reading of
748	"reduce per-arm boilerplate" and `linkme` / `inventory` are
749	the higher-leverage / higher-cost alternatives queued as
750	follow-up todos.
751	
752	### Why not linkme / inventory now
753	
754	Both eliminate the `Commands` enum entirely (link-time
755	distributed slice or runtime registry). They'd cut
756	per-subcommand touchpoints from 3 (mod decl + enum variant +
757	match arm) to 1 (registration). Costs: compile-time
758	exhaustiveness check goes away (missing registration =
759	runtime gap); help-output ordering becomes link-order
760	unless sorted; either is macro-magic dependency. The trait
761	sweep gets every match arm to one line with none of those
762	costs; linkme + inventory are queued as separate Todo items
763	to revisit if the per-arm cost ever feels burdensome.
764	
765	### Per-substep contract
766	
767	Per `notes/substep-protocol.md`: `cargo fmt` / `clippy
768	--all-targets -- -D warnings` / `test` / `install --path .
769	--locked` + retest before each commit; bump `Cargo.toml` at
770	sub-step start; flip todo ladder markers; pair commits
771	across both repos with ochid trailers.
772	
773	## refactor: SubcommandRunner trait + chid (0.50.0-1)
774	
775	Commits: [[14]]
776	
777	Worked example for the 0.50.0 cycle: introduce the
778	`SubcommandRunner` trait, validate its shape on `chid`, and
779	extract `main.rs`'s session-chrome into a global `sb_ide`
780	function consumed both by the trait's default `dispatch`
781	(for ported commands) and from `main` directly (for
782	unported arms). The Chid arm in `main` reaches its target
783	one-liner — `Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(cli.log),`
784	— with chid's banner suppression riding on a new
785	`suppress_banner` field in `ChidParams` that the trait's
786	default `dispatch` reads via
787	`SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner(&params)`.
788	
789	Three material design calls landed at this substep's
790	evaluation gate (full discussion below): the trait was
791	renamed from `Subcommand` to `SubcommandRunner` to sidestep
792	a `clap::Subcommand` collision; the session-chrome block
793	(banner + `surface_previous_failures`) was extracted into a
794	global `pub fn sb_ide` in `main.rs`; and the trait's
795	`suppress_banner` / `is_detached_exec` peeks were *kept* on
796	the trait — default `false`, signature `(_params:
797	&Self::Params)` — with the chrome data living on each
798	command's `Params` (e.g. `ChidParams::suppress_banner`) so
```

1	//! `SubcommandRunner` trait
2	//!
3	//! The purpose is to simplify invoking a subcommand so `main`
4	//! can invoke any subcommand with one line:
5	//!
6	//! `Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(cli.log),`
7	//!
8	//! Instead of the ~16 lines that the default `dispatch` below
9	//! now encapsulates.
10	//!
11	//! Implementors provide:
12	//!
13	//! - [`SubcommandRunner::to_params`] — build the clap-free
14	//!   `Params` from the clap `Args` (covers both `From` /
15	//!   `TryFrom` shapes uniformly via `Result<_, String>`).
16	//! - [`SubcommandRunner::run`] — the subcommand body.
17	//!
18	//! The default [`SubcommandRunner::dispatch`] loads `Context`,
19	//! builds `Params` via `to_params`, runs via `run`, and maps
20	//! the result to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via
21	//! `error!` and return `ExitCode::FAILURE`.
22	//!
23	//! ## See also
24	//!
25	//! - The wider CLI-args / subcommand-layer split this trait
26	//!   formalizes: [`ARCHITECTURE.md > args → Context + Params`][arch].
27	//! - Cycle design, ladder, evaluation gate:
28	//!   [`chores-10.md > 0.50.0-0`][open].
29	//! - Worked example, trait-shape decisions, naming:
30	//!   [`chores-10.md > 0.50.0-1`][port].
31	//! - First implementor: [`crate::chid`].
32	//!
33	//! [arch]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/ARCHITECTURE.md#args--context--params
34	//! [open]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/notes/chores-10.md#chore-open-subcommand-trait-sweep-0500-0
35	//! [port]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/notes/chores-10.md#refactor-subcommandrunner-trait--chid-0500-1
36	
37	use std::path::PathBuf;
38	use std::process::ExitCode;
39	
40	use log::error;
41	
42	use crate::context::Context;
43	
44	/// Trait implemented by every subcommand's clap `Args` type so
45	/// `main.rs` can dispatch via a single `args.dispatch(cli.log)`
46	/// call.
47	pub trait SubcommandRunner {
48	    /// The clap-free `Params` struct the subcommand body
49	    /// consumes.
50	    type Params;
51	
52	    /// Build `Params` from `Args`. `Result<_, String>` absorbs
53	    /// both `From` (return `Ok(...)`) and `TryFrom` (forward
54	    /// the error) shapes uniformly.
55	    fn to_params(&self) -> Result<Self::Params, String>;
56	
57	    /// Run the subcommand body.
58	    fn run(ctx: &Context, params: &Self::Params) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>>;
59	
60	    /// Whether to suppress the leading `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner.
61	    /// Default `false`; commands whose `Params` carry a
62	    /// banner-suppression flag (e.g. `chid` / `desc` / `list` /
63	    /// `show` under `-L`) override to read it.
64	    fn suppress_banner(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
65	        false
66	    }
67	
68	    /// Whether this invocation is the detached `finalize --exec`
69	    /// child. Default `false`; `finalize` (when ported) overrides
70	    /// to read it from its `Params`.
71	    fn is_detached_exec(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
72	        false
73	    }
74	
75	    /// Default dispatch: load `Context`, build `Params` via
76	    /// `to_params`, emit session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`],
77	    /// run via `run`, and map the result to `ExitCode`. Errors
78	    /// at any stage log via `error!` and return
79	    /// `ExitCode::FAILURE`.
80	    fn dispatch(&self, log: Option<PathBuf>) -> ExitCode {
81	        let ctx = match Context::load(log) {
82	            Ok(c) => c,
83	            Err(e) => {
84	                error!("{e}");
85	                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
86	            }
87	        };
88	        let params = match self.to_params() {
89	            Ok(p) => p,
90	            Err(e) => {
91	                error!("{e}");
92	                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
93	            }
94	        };
95	        crate::sb_ide(
96	            Self::suppress_banner(&params),
97	            Self::is_detached_exec(&params),
98	        );
99	        match Self::run(&ctx, &params) {
100	            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
101	            Err(e) => {
102	                error!("{e}");
103	                ExitCode::FAILURE
104	            }
105	        }
106	    }
107	}
108	
```

1	//! `chid` subcommand — print the short change ID of each commit in a
2	//! revision range, one per line (script-friendly output).
3	//!
4	//! - `ChidArgs`: clap surface; flattens
5	//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs`.
6	//! - `ChidParams`: clap-free; embeds `common::CommonParams`. Built
7	//!   via `TryFrom<&ChidArgs>` at the binary edge.
8	//! - `chid(&Context, &ChidParams)`: the op — `for_each_repo` + print
9	//!   `format_chid`.
10	
11	use clap::Args;
12	use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
13	use log::{debug, info};
14	
15	use crate::common::{self, CommonParams};
16	use crate::context::Context;
17	use crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;
18	use crate::subcommand::SubcommandRunner;
19	
20	/// CLI args for `chid` — just the shared read-only commit-query args.
21	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
22	pub struct ChidArgs {
23	    #[command(flatten)]
24	    pub common: CommonArgs,
25	}
26	
27	/// Clap-free params for `chid`; embeds resolved `CommonParams`.
28	///
29	/// Carries a `suppress_banner` flag (read off `-L` / `--no-label`
30	/// at the binary edge) so the trait's default `dispatch` can
31	/// query it through `SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner` without
32	/// re-touching clap.
33	#[derive(Debug)]
34	pub struct ChidParams {
35	    pub common: CommonParams,
36	    pub suppress_banner: bool,
37	}
38	
39	impl TryFrom<&ChidArgs> for ChidParams {
40	    type Error = String;
41	
42	    /// Resolve clap `ChidArgs` into `ChidParams` by delegating to
43	    /// `CommonParams::try_from` and copying the no-label flag
44	    /// into `suppress_banner`.
45	    fn try_from(a: &ChidArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {
46	        Ok(ChidParams {
47	            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,
48	            suppress_banner: a.common.no_label,
49	        })
50	    }
51	}
52	
53	/// Print the short change ID of each commit in the resolved range.
54	///
55	/// `_ctx` is unused (chid has no user-config or `--log` consumer);
56	/// it's present for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.
57	pub fn chid(_ctx: &Context, params: &ChidParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
58	    debug!("chid: enter");
59	    let c = &params.common;
60	    common::for_each_repo(&c.repos, &c.header, |workspace, repo| {
61	        let (ids, _) = common::collect_ids(
62	            workspace,
63	            repo,
64	            &c.spec.rev,
65	            c.spec.desc_count,
66	            c.spec.anc_count,
67	        )?;
68	        for commit_id in &ids {
69	            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
70	            info!("{}", common::format_chid(&commit));
71	        }
72	        Ok(())
73	    })?;
74	    debug!("chid: exit");
75	    Ok(())
76	}
77	
78	impl SubcommandRunner for ChidArgs {
79	    type Params = ChidParams;
80	
81	    /// Delegate to the existing `TryFrom<&ChidArgs>` impl above.
82	    fn to_params(&self) -> Result<Self::Params, String> {
83	        ChidParams::try_from(self)
84	    }
85	
86	    /// Run the existing `chid` op.
87	    fn run(ctx: &Context, params: &Self::Params) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
88	        chid(ctx, params)
89	    }
90	
91	    /// Read the banner-suppression flag off `ChidParams` for
92	    /// `crate::sb_ide` (queried from the trait's default
93	    /// `dispatch`).
94	    fn suppress_banner(params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
95	        params.suppress_banner
96	    }
97	}
98

1	//! `desc` subcommand — print each commit in a revision range with
2	//! its full description (changeID + commitID + bookmarks + title,
3	//! then the body), the anchor commit bolded.
4	//!
5	//! - `DescArgs`: clap surface; flattens
6	//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs`.
7	//! - `DescParams`: clap-free; embeds `common::CommonParams`. Built
8	//!   via `TryFrom<&DescArgs>` at the binary edge.
9	//! - `desc(&Context, &DescParams)`: the op — `for_each_repo` +
10	//!   `format_commit_full` + `indent_body`.
11	
12	use clap::Args;
13	use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
14	use log::{debug, info};
15	
16	use crate::common::{self, CommonParams};
17	use crate::context::Context;
18	use crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;
19	
20	/// CLI args for `desc` — just the shared read-only commit-query args.
21	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
22	pub struct DescArgs {
23	    #[command(flatten)]
24	    pub common: CommonArgs,
25	}
26	
27	/// Clap-free params for `desc`; embeds resolved `CommonParams`.
28	#[derive(Debug)]
29	pub struct DescParams {
30	    pub common: CommonParams,
31	}
32	
33	impl TryFrom<&DescArgs> for DescParams {
34	    type Error = String;
35	
36	    /// Resolve clap `DescArgs` into `DescParams` by delegating to
37	    /// `CommonParams::try_from`; `desc` has no fields beyond
38	    /// `CommonArgs`.
39	    fn try_from(a: &DescArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {
40	        Ok(DescParams {
41	            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,
42	        })
43	    }
44	}
45	
46	/// Print each commit in the resolved range with its full description.
47	///
48	/// `_ctx` is unused (desc has no user-config or `--log` consumer);
49	/// it's present for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.
50	pub fn desc(_ctx: &Context, params: &DescParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
51	    debug!("desc: enter");
52	    let c = &params.common;
53	    common::for_each_repo(&c.repos, &c.header, |workspace, repo| {
54	        let (ids, anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
55	            workspace,
56	            repo,
57	            &c.spec.rev,
58	            c.spec.desc_count,
59	            c.spec.anc_count,
60	        )?;
61	        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
62	            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
63	            let bookmarks = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, commit_id);
64	            let line = common::format_commit_full(&commit, &bookmarks);
65	            let indented = common::indent_body(&line, 4);
66	            if i == anchor_index {
67	                info!("{}", common::bold_first_line(&indented));
68	            } else {
69	                info!("{indented}");
70	            }
71	        }
72	        Ok(())
73	    })?;
74	    debug!("desc: exit");
75	    Ok(())
76	}
77

38	/// Banner string emitted as the first line of normal command runs
39	/// and shown at the top of subcommand `--help` output. Built from
40	/// `Cargo.toml`'s name + version at compile time so it stays in
41	/// sync with the bumped version.
42	const BANNER: &str = concat!(env!("CARGO_PKG_NAME"), " ", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"));
43	
44	/// Top-level about line — name, version, and the project tagline
45	/// on a single line. Used as the top-level `about` so `vc-x1 -h`
46	/// reads as one banner-plus-tagline header instead of two stacked
47	/// lines.
48	const TOP_ABOUT: &str = concat!(
49	    env!("CARGO_PKG_NAME"),
50	    " ",
51	    env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"),
52	    " - jj workspace tooling"
53	);
54	
55	/// Build the clap command tree with `BANNER` set as `before_help`
56	/// on every subcommand (transitively). Top-level skips `before_help`
57	/// because its own `about` already carries the name+version+tagline.
58	/// Walks via `mut_subcommand` so individual subcommand
59	/// `#[command(long_about = ...)]` blocks don't have to repeat the
60	/// banner text.
61	fn cli_with_banner() -> clap::Command {
62	    fn add_to_subs(mut cmd: clap::Command) -> clap::Command {
63	        let names: Vec<String> = cmd
64	            .get_subcommands()
65	            .map(|c| c.get_name().to_string())
66	            .collect();
67	        for name in names {
68	            cmd = cmd.mut_subcommand(name, add_with_banner);
69	        }
70	        cmd
71	    }
72	    fn add_with_banner(mut cmd: clap::Command) -> clap::Command {
73	        cmd = cmd.before_help(BANNER);
74	        let names: Vec<String> = cmd
75	            .get_subcommands()
76	            .map(|c| c.get_name().to_string())
77	            .collect();
78	        for name in names {
79	            cmd = cmd.mut_subcommand(name, add_with_banner);
80	        }
```

> AGENT

Good — the hoist builds. Two observations:

1. `dispatch` now takes `ctx: Context` by value but only passes `&ctx` to `run`. Since every match arm uses `&ctx`, taking `&Context` would be consistent with the rest of the codebase (chid, desc, etc. all take `&Context`) and avoid the move.

2. The commented-out blocks (`//let ctx = …`) and the `//fn dispatch(&self /* , log: …*/)` line — those are exploration scaffolding. They should be deleted once we settle on the shape.

Want me to:
- (a) change dispatch to take `&Context`, OR
- (b) keep `Context` by value (move semantics work — only one arm runs)

And then cleanup the commented-out scaffolding either way?

1	//! `SubcommandRunner` trait
2	//!
3	//! The purpose is to simplify invoking a subcommand so `main`
4	//! can invoke any subcommand with one line:
5	//!
6	//! `Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(cli.log),`
7	//!
8	//! Instead of the ~16 lines that the default `dispatch` below
9	//! now encapsulates.
10	//!
11	//! Implementors provide:
12	//!
13	//! - [`SubcommandRunner::to_params`] — build the clap-free
14	//!   `Params` from the clap `Args` (covers both `From` /
15	//!   `TryFrom` shapes uniformly via `Result<_, String>`).
16	//! - [`SubcommandRunner::run`] — the subcommand body.
17	//!
18	//! The default [`SubcommandRunner::dispatch`] loads `Context`,
19	//! builds `Params` via `to_params`, runs via `run`, and maps
20	//! the result to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via
21	//! `error!` and return `ExitCode::FAILURE`.
22	//!
23	//! ## See also
24	//!
25	//! - The wider CLI-args / subcommand-layer split this trait
26	//!   formalizes: [`ARCHITECTURE.md > args → Context + Params`][arch].
27	//! - Cycle design, ladder, evaluation gate:
28	//!   [`chores-10.md > 0.50.0-0`][open].
29	//! - Worked example, trait-shape decisions, naming:
30	//!   [`chores-10.md > 0.50.0-1`][port].
31	//! - First implementor: [`crate::chid`].
32	//!
33	//! [arch]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/ARCHITECTURE.md#args--context--params
34	//! [open]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/notes/chores-10.md#chore-open-subcommand-trait-sweep-0500-0
35	//! [port]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/notes/chores-10.md#refactor-subcommandrunner-trait--chid-0500-1
36	
37	//use std::path::PathBuf;
38	use std::process::ExitCode;
39	
40	use log::error;
41	
42	use crate::context::Context;
43	
44	/// Trait implemented by every subcommand's clap `Args` type so
45	/// `main.rs` can dispatch via a single `args.dispatch(cli.log)`
46	/// call.
47	pub trait SubcommandRunner {
48	    /// The clap-free `Params` struct the subcommand body
49	    /// consumes.
50	    type Params;
51	
52	    /// Build `Params` from `Args`. `Result<_, String>` absorbs
53	    /// both `From` (return `Ok(...)`) and `TryFrom` (forward
54	    /// the error) shapes uniformly.
55	    fn to_params(&self) -> Result<Self::Params, String>;
56	
57	    /// Run the subcommand body.
58	    fn run(ctx: &Context, params: &Self::Params) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>>;
59	
60	    /// Whether to suppress the leading `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner.
61	    /// Default `false`; commands whose `Params` carry a
62	    /// banner-suppression flag (e.g. `chid` / `desc` / `list` /
63	    /// `show` under `-L`) override to read it.
64	    fn suppress_banner(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
65	        false
66	    }
67	
68	    /// Whether this invocation is the detached `finalize --exec`
69	    /// child. Default `false`; `finalize` (when ported) overrides
70	    /// to read it from its `Params`.
71	    fn is_detached_exec(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
72	        false
73	    }
74	
75	    /// Default dispatch: load `Context`, build `Params` via
76	    /// `to_params`, emit session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`],
77	    /// run via `run`, and map the result to `ExitCode`. Errors
78	    /// at any stage log via `error!` and return
79	    /// `ExitCode::FAILURE`.
80	    //fn dispatch(&self /* , log: Option<PathBuf>*/) -> ExitCode {
81	    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {
82	        //let ctx = match Context::load(log) {
83	        //    Ok(c) => c,
84	        //    Err(e) => {
85	        //        error!("{e}");
86	        //        return ExitCode::FAILURE;
87	        //    }
88	        //};
89	        let params = match self.to_params() {
90	            Ok(p) => p,
91	            Err(e) => {
92	                error!("{e}");
93	                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
94	            }
95	        };
96	        crate::sb_ide(
97	            Self::suppress_banner(&params),
98	            Self::is_detached_exec(&params),
99	        );
100	        match Self::run(&ctx, &params) {
101	            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
102	            Err(e) => {
103	                error!("{e}");
104	                ExitCode::FAILURE
105	            }
106	        }
107	    }
108	}
109	
```

The purpose is to simplify invoking a subcommand so `main`\n//! can invoke any subcommand with one line:\n//!\n//! `Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),`\n//!\n//! Instead of the ~11 lines that the default `dispatch` below\n//! now encapsulates.\n//!\n//! Implementors provide:\n//!\n//! - [`SubcommandRunner::to_params`] — build the clap-free\n//!   `Params` from the clap `Args` (covers both `From` /\n//!   `TryFrom` shapes uniformly via `Result<_, String>`).\n//! - [`SubcommandRunner::run`] — the subcommand body.\n//!\n//! The default [`SubcommandRunner::dispatch`] builds `Params`\n//! via `to_params`, runs via `run`, and maps the result to\n//! `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via\n    /// `error!` and return `ExitCode::FAILURE`.\n    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {\n        let params = match self.to_params() {\n            Ok(p) => p,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                return ExitCode::FAILURE;\n            }\n        };\n        crate::sb_ide(\n            Self::suppress_banner(&params),\n            Self::is_detached_exec(&params),\n        );\n        match Self::run(ctx, &params) {\n            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                ExitCode::FAILURE\n            }\n        }\n    }"
}

1050 notes/chores-10.md
---

## chore: --exec doc + matches! → match (0.50.0-1.2)

Inter-substep tidy: a small readability rewrite in
`main.rs` and a doc expansion on `FinalizeArgs::exec` so
the `--detach` / `--exec` handshake is spelled out at
the field that implements it.

- `src/main.rs`: `let is_detached_exec = matches!(cli.command,
  Commands::Finalize(ref f) if f.exec)` → explicit
  two-arm `match`. Semantically identical (both expand to
  the same thing); the trailing-`if`-guard inside
  `matches!` was found weird enough to be worth replacing
  at this site.
- `src/finalize.rs`: `FinalizeArgs::exec` doc expanded
  from the one-liner "Internal: run exec path (used by
  detach)" to a paragraph anchored on the sibling `pub
  detach` field — when `--detach` is passed the parent
  `fork`s and re-execs `vc-x1 finalize --exec …`, the
  child sees `exec: true` and routes through
  `finalize_exec()` for the background work — plus a
  second paragraph spelling out why `hide = true` is set.

  "new_string": "  \"**Audit hardcoded `.claude` in diagnostics /\n  logging.**\" entry inserted just below them.\n\n## refactor: hoist Context::load (0.50.0-2)\n\n`Context::load` was invoked in every match arm — once\nin `dispatch` for the ported `Chid` arm, and inline at\nthe head of each of the 10 unported arms. The body is\nidentical, the result is identical, only one arm runs\nper invocation. Hoist the load to a single site at the\ntop of the match in `main`, change\n`SubcommandRunner::dispatch` to take `&Context` instead\nof building one itself, and let the unported arms read\nthe same `&ctx`. Net `-63` lines in `main.rs`.\n\nThe trait shape change is small but load-bearing for\nthe rest of the cycle — every future port arrives at the\nsettled `dispatch(&ctx)` shape rather than the\n`dispatch(cli.log)` shape. Work complete. Summary of changes:

**App repo:**
- `Cargo.toml`: `0.50.0-1.2` → `0.50.0-2`
- `src/main.rs`: `Context::load` hoisted out of every match arm into a single block above the match; 10 inline blocks deleted (net −63 lines)
- `src/subcommand.rs`: `dispatch(&self, log: Option<PathBuf>)` → `dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context)`; inline `Context::load` removed; module + item docs updated; unused `PathBuf` import dropped
- `notes/chores-10.md`: backfilled `Commits: [[16]]` on the 0.50.0-1.2 section + added [16] ref; new `## refactor: hoist Context::load (0.50.0-2)` section
- `notes/todo.md`: ladder updated — `0.50.0-2 hoist Context::load + dispatch(&Context) (current)` + new `0.50.0-3..N` placeholder

**Cargo cycle:** fmt ✓ clippy --all-targets -D warnings ✓ test ✓ install --locked ✓ smoke (chid ported + desc unported both work with `-L`) ✓

Proposed commit title and body for `vc-x1 push main`:

```
refactor: hoist Context::load (0.50.0-2)
```

```
Lift `Context::load` out of every per-arm body in
`main.rs` into a single site above the match; change
`SubcommandRunner::dispatch` to take `&Context` instead
of building one itself. Net -63 lines in `main.rs`.

Splitting the hoist out as its own substep keeps the
upcoming per-port commits (starting with `desc` at
`-3`) reviewable in isolation — each one becomes "add
`suppress_banner` + impl `SubcommandRunner` + collapse
the arm" with no infrastructure churn mixed in. See
`notes/chores-10.md > refactor: hoist Context::load
(0.50.0-2)` for the design discussion.

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0-1.2 → 0.50.0-2.
- src/main.rs: Context::load lifted into a single
  block above `match cli.command`; 10 inline blocks
  deleted.
- src/subcommand.rs: dispatch signature changed to
  `dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context)`; inline
  Context::load deleted; module/item doc-comments
  updated for the new signature; unused
  `std::path::PathBuf` import deleted.
- notes/chores-10.md: backfilled Commits: [[16]] on
  the 0.50.0-1.2 section; new section "refactor:
  hoist Context::load (0.50.0-2)".
- notes/todo.md: ladder updated — 0.50.0-2 hoist
  (current); 0.50.0-3..N placeholder added.
```

Please review. On approval I'll run `vc-x1 push main`.

> DEVELOPER

LG, and the commit description is ok, but I'd like to shorten it and remove the "sub-statement" following the semi-colon in each, I don't think the extra detail is necessary. Thoughts?

> AGENT

Agreed — the post-semicolon details are mostly consequences of the main change in each bullet. For `subcommand.rs`, dropping the sub-statements gives "dispatch signature changed to `dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context)`" which is the load-bearing change; the inline-`Context::load` removal and doc updates are downstream of that and the diff itself shows them.

Trimmed body:

```
Lift `Context::load` out of every per-arm body in
`main.rs` into a single site above the match; change
`SubcommandRunner::dispatch` to take `&Context` instead
of building one itself. Net -63 lines in `main.rs`.

Splitting the hoist out as its own substep keeps the
upcoming per-port commits (starting with `desc` at
`-3`) reviewable in isolation — each one becomes "add
`suppress_banner` + impl `SubcommandRunner` + collapse
the arm" with no infrastructure churn mixed in. See
`notes/chores-10.md > refactor: hoist Context::load
(0.50.0-2)` for the design discussion.

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0-1.2 → 0.50.0-2.
- src/main.rs: Context::load lifted into a single
  block above `match cli.command`.
- src/subcommand.rs: dispatch signature changed to
  `dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context)`.
- notes/chores-10.md: backfilled Commits: [[16]] on
  the 0.50.0-1.2 section.
- notes/todo.md: ladder updated — 0.50.0-2 hoist
  (current).
```

  "new_string": "use clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\nuse log::{debug, info};\n\nuse crate::common::{self, CommonParams};\nuse crate::context::Context;\nuse crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;\nuse crate::subcommand::SubcommandRunner;\n\n/// CLI args for `desc` — just the shared read-only commit-query args.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct DescArgs {\n    #[command(flatten)]\n    pub common: CommonArgs,\n}\n\n/// Clap-free params for `desc`; embeds resolved `CommonParams`.\n///\n/// Carries a `suppress_banner` flag (read off `-L` / `--no-label`\n/// at the binary edge) so the trait's default `dispatch` can\n/// query it through `SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner` without\n/// re-touching clap.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct DescParams {\n    pub common: CommonParams,\n    pub suppress_banner: bool,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&DescArgs> for DescParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Resolve clap `DescArgs` into `DescParams` by delegating to\n    /// `CommonParams::try_from` and copying the no-label flag\n    /// into `suppress_banner`.\n    fn try_from(a: &DescArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        Ok(DescParams {\n            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,\n            suppress_banner: a.common.no_label,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\nimpl SubcommandRunner for DescArgs {\n    type Params = DescParams;\n\n    /// Delegate to the existing `TryFrom<&DescArgs>` impl above.\n    fn to_params(&self) -> Result<Self::Params, String> {\n        DescParams::try_from(self)\n    }\n\n    /// Run the existing `desc` op.\n    fn run(ctx: &Context, params: &Self::Params) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n        desc(ctx, params)\n    }\n\n    /// Read the banner-suppression flag off `DescParams` for\n    /// `crate::sb_ide` (queried from the trait's default\n    /// `dispatch`).\n    fn suppress_banner(params: &Self::Params) -> bool {\n        params.suppress_banner\n    }\n}"
}

```
First port off the `chid` worked example: `desc` lands
on the trait-based `dispatch` shape settled in `-2`.
The arm in `main.rs` collapses to
`Commands::Desc(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),`, and
`desc` joins `chid` in the trait-owned banner-suppression
path (so `desc -L` no longer needs a special-case match
arm at the top of `main`).

Mechanical, no design calls — the shape mirrors `chid`
(0.50.0-1) exactly. See
`notes/chores-10.md > refactor: desc → SubcommandRunner
(0.50.0-3)` for the full record.

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0-2 → 0.50.0-3.
- src/desc.rs: `SubcommandRunner for DescArgs` impl
  added; `suppress_banner: bool` field on `DescParams`.
- src/main.rs: `Desc` arm collapsed to
  `args.dispatch(&ctx)`.
- notes/chores-10.md: backfilled `Commits: [[17]]`
  on the 0.50.0-2 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.50.0-2 → (done) (absorbing missed
  flip); 0.50.0-3 port desc (current); placeholder
  trimmed to "remaining 10".
```

id: toolu_01VQmGrDgKB81ztGsyArZF8i
```
1	//! `list` subcommand — list commits in a jj repo, one per line:
2	//! changeID + ochid (padded) + bookmarks + title, the anchor bolded.
3	//!
4	//! - `ListArgs`: clap surface; flattens
5	//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs` plus a `-w`/`--width`
6	//!   for the ochid column.
7	//! - `ListParams`: clap-free; embeds `common::CommonParams` + `width`.
8	//!   Built via `TryFrom<&ListArgs>` at the binary edge.
9	//! - `list(&Context, &ListParams)`: the op — `for_each_repo` +
10	//!   `format_commit_with_ochid`.
11	
12	use clap::Args;
13	use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
14	use log::{debug, info, trace, warn};
15	
16	use crate::common::{self, CommonParams};
17	use crate::context::Context;
18	use crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;
19	
20	/// CLI args for `list` — the shared read-only commit-query args plus
21	/// the ochid column width.
22	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
23	pub struct ListArgs {
24	    #[command(flatten)]
25	    pub common: CommonArgs,
26	
27	    /// ochid column width
28	    #[arg(short = 'w', long = "width", default_value_t = DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH)]
29	    pub width: usize,
30	}
31	
32	const DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH: usize = 21;
33	
34	/// Clap-free params for `list`; embeds `CommonParams` and the ochid
35	/// column width.
36	#[derive(Debug)]
37	pub struct ListParams {
38	    pub common: CommonParams,
39	    pub width: usize,
40	}
41	
42	impl TryFrom<&ListArgs> for ListParams {
43	    type Error = String;
44	
45	    /// Resolve clap `ListArgs` into `ListParams`: delegate to
46	    /// `CommonParams::try_from` for the shared fields; copy `width`
47	    /// straight over (clap-applied default already resolved).
48	    fn try_from(a: &ListArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {
49	        Ok(ListParams {
50	            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,
51	            width: a.width,
52	        })
53	    }
54	}
55	
56	/// List commits in the resolved range with the ochid column.
57	///
58	/// `_ctx` is unused (list has no user-config or `--log` consumer);
59	/// it's present for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.
60	pub fn list(_ctx: &Context, params: &ListParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
61	    debug!("list: enter");
62	    let c = &params.common;
63	    trace!(
64	        "list: spec rev={} desc={:?} anc={:?}",
65	        c.spec.rev, c.spec.desc_count, c.spec.anc_count
66	    );
67	
68	    common::for_each_repo(&c.repos, &c.header, |workspace, repo| {
69	        let (ids, anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
70	            workspace,
71	            repo,
72	            &c.spec.rev,
73	            c.spec.desc_count,
74	            c.spec.anc_count,
75	        )?;
76	        debug!("list: {} commits, anchor at {anchor_index}", ids.len());
77	
78	        if ids.is_empty() {
79	            warn!("list: no commits found for revision '{}'", c.spec.rev);
80	        }

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/list.rs",
  "old_string": "use clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\nuse log::{debug, info, trace, warn};\n\nuse crate::common::{self, CommonParams};\nuse crate::context::Context;\nuse crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;\n\n/// CLI args for `list` — the shared read-only commit-query args plus\n/// the ochid column width.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct ListArgs {\n    #[command(flatten)]\n    pub common: CommonArgs,\n\n    /// ochid column width\n    #[arg(short = 'w', long = \"width\", default_value_t = DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH)]\n    pub width: usize,\n}\n\nconst DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH: usize = 21;\n\n/// Clap-free params for `list`; embeds `CommonParams` and the ochid\n/// column width.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct ListParams {\n    pub common: CommonParams,\n    pub width: usize,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&ListArgs> for ListParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Resolve clap `ListArgs` into `ListParams`: delegate to\n    /// `CommonParams::try_from` for the shared fields; copy `width`\n    /// straight over (clap-applied default already resolved).\n    fn try_from(a: &ListArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        Ok(ListParams {\n            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,\n            width: a.width,\n        })\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "use clap::Args;\nuse jj_lib::repo::Repo;\nuse log::{debug, info, trace, warn};\n\nuse crate::common::{self, CommonParams};\nuse crate::context::Context;\nuse crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;\nuse crate::subcommand::SubcommandRunner;\n\n/// CLI args for `list` — the shared read-only commit-query args plus\n/// the ochid column width.\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct ListArgs {\n    #[command(flatten)]\n    pub common: CommonArgs,\n\n    /// ochid column width\n    #[arg(short = 'w', long = \"width\", default_value_t = DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH)]\n    pub width: usize,\n}\n\nconst DEFAULT_OCHID_WIDTH: usize = 21;\n\n/// Clap-free params for `list`; embeds `CommonParams` and the ochid\n/// column width.\n///\n/// Carries a `suppress_banner` flag (read off `-L` / `--no-label`\n/// at the binary edge) so the trait's default `dispatch` can\n/// query it through `SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner` without\n/// re-touching clap.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct ListParams {\n    pub common: CommonParams,\n    pub width: usize,\n    pub suppress_banner: bool,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&ListArgs> for ListParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Resolve clap `ListArgs` into `ListParams`: delegate to\n    /// `CommonParams::try_from` for the shared fields; copy `width`\n    /// straight over (clap-applied default already resolved); copy\n    /// the no-label flag into `suppress_banner`.\n    fn try_from(a: &ListArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        Ok(ListParams {\n            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,\n            width: a.width,\n            suppress_banner: a.common.no_label,\n        })\n    }\n}\n\nimpl SubcommandRunner for ListArgs {\n    type Params = ListParams;\n\n    /// Delegate to the existing `TryFrom<&ListArgs>` impl above.\n    fn to_params(&self) -> Result<Self::Params, String> {\n        ListParams::try_from(self)\n    }\n\n    /// Run the existing `list` op.\n    fn run(ctx: &Context, params: &Self::Params) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n        list(ctx, params)\n    }\n\n    /// Read the banner-suppression flag off `ListParams` for\n    /// `crate::sb_ide` (queried from the trait's default\n    /// `dispatch`).\n    fn suppress_banner(params: &Self::Params) -> bool {\n        params.suppress_banner\n    }\n}"
}

Skip the banner\n    // whenever the active subcommand has the flag set. `chid` and\n    // `desc` are omitted because they're already ported to\n    // `SubcommandRunner::dispatch` (which reads suppression off\n    // each command's `Params`); each future port removes its\n    // variant here.\n    let suppress_banner = match &cli.command {\n        Commands::List(a) => a.common.no_label,\n        Commands::Show(a) => a.common.no_label,\n        _ => false,\n    };",
  "new_string": "    // `-L` / `--no-label` on the read-only multi-repo subcommand\n    // `show` makes its output script-parseable — adding a leading\n    // `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` line would break that. Skip the banner whenever\n    // the active subcommand has the flag set. `chid`, `desc`, and\n    // `list` are omitted because they're already ported to\n    // `SubcommandRunner::dispatch` (which reads suppression off\n    // each command's `Params`); each future port removes its\n    // variant here.\n    let suppress_banner = match &cli.command {\n        Commands::Show(a) => a.common.no_label,\n        _ => false,\n    };"
}

> DEVELOPER

This is pretty mechanical, any reason not to do the remainder in one commit?

> AGENT

No real reason to keep splitting — the remaining 9 ports are the same edit pattern × 9, and the diff would be boring but reviewable in one pass. The only nuance is that **finalize** also needs `is_detached_exec` plumbed through the trait (the trait already has the override slot, just no implementor uses it yet), but that's still mechanical.

My suggested shape:

- **0.50.0-5** — all 9 remaining ports + K cleanup (delete the top-level `is_detached_exec` / `suppress_banner` matches and the `sb_ide()` call from `main`, since every arm now dispatches through the trait).
- **0.50.0** (no suffix) — cycle close-out: move todo entry to Done, add `## chore: close Subcommand trait sweep` section.

That collapses 9 mechanical substeps + K + close-out into 2 commits. The main tradeoff vs. continuing one-at-a-time is bisect granularity — if one of the 9 ports introduces a regression, the failing commit names "all 9 ports + K" rather than a single subcommand. But ports are independent and the test suite covers each subcommand, so that risk is small.

OK to proceed with that shape, or do you want to keep per-port?

> DEVELOPER

let's have 0.50.0-5 do the all except for finalise, let's do it separately as it's the weirdest subcommand

> AGENT

Good call — finalize's `is_detached_exec` plumbing is its own micro-decision. Starting 0.50.0-5: port show + the 7 From commands (validate_desc, fix_desc, clone, init, symlink, sync, push).

6	//!   `options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs` plus a `-f`/`--files`
7	//!   cap (raw `String`; parsed at the boundary into [`FileLimit`]).
8	//! - `ShowParams`: clap-free; embeds `common::CommonParams` and the
9	//!   parsed `FileLimit`. Built via `TryFrom<&ShowArgs>` at the
10	//!   binary edge.
11	//! - `show(&Context, &ShowParams)`: the op — `for_each_repo`, one
12	//!   `show_one_commit` per commit.
13	
14	use std::sync::Arc;
15	
16	use chrono::Offset;
17	use clap::Args;
18	use jj_lib::backend::TreeValue;
19	use jj_lib::commit::Commit;
20	use jj_lib::matchers::EverythingMatcher;
21	use jj_lib::merged_tree::TreeDiffIterator;
22	use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
23	use jj_lib::repo::{ReadonlyRepo, Repo};
24	use jj_lib::workspace::Workspace;
25	use pollster::FutureExt;
26	
27	use log::{debug, info};
28	
29	use crate::common::{self, CommonParams};
30	use crate::context::Context;
31	use crate::options_flags::common_args::CommonArgs;
32	
33	/// Parsed file limit: None (suppress), Some(n) (cap at n), or all.
34	#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
35	pub enum FileLimit {
36	    /// Don't show changed files section at all.
37	    None,
38	    /// Show up to N files (with first/last split if truncated).
39	    Cap(usize),
40	    /// Show all files.
41	    All,
42	}
43	
44	impl FileLimit {
45	    /// Parse the `--files` flag string into a `FileLimit` — `"0"` →
46	    /// None, `"all"` → All, otherwise a positive integer → Cap(n).
47	    /// Called at the boundary by `ShowParams::try_from`.
48	    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {
49	        match s {
50	            "0" => Ok(FileLimit::None),
51	            "all" => Ok(FileLimit::All),
52	            _ => s
53	                .parse::<usize>()
54	                .map(FileLimit::Cap)
55	                .map_err(|_| format!("invalid file limit '{s}': expected number, 0, or 'all'")),
56	        }
57	    }
58	}
59	
60	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
61	pub struct ShowArgs {
62	    #[command(flatten)]
63	    pub common: CommonArgs,
64	
65	    /// Max changed files: number, 0 (none), or 'all'
66	    #[arg(short = 'f', long = "files", default_value = "50")]
67	    pub files: String,
68	}
69	
70	/// Clap-free params for `show`; embeds `CommonParams` plus the parsed
71	/// `FileLimit`.
72	#[derive(Debug)]
73	pub struct ShowParams {
74	    pub common: CommonParams,
75	    pub files: FileLimit,
76	}
77	
78	impl TryFrom<&ShowArgs> for ShowParams {
79	    type Error = String;
80	
81	    /// Resolve clap `ShowArgs` into `ShowParams`: delegate to
82	    /// `CommonParams::try_from` for the shared fields and parse
83	    /// `--files` into `FileLimit` at the boundary. Fallible for two
84	    /// reasons (`resolve_repos` or `FileLimit::parse`); both errors
85	    /// surface as `String` for uniform handling in `main`.
86	    fn try_from(a: &ShowArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {
87	        Ok(ShowParams {
88	            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,
89	            files: FileLimit::parse(&a.files)?,
90	        })
91	    }
92	}
93	
94	/// Print full details + a changed-files summary for each commit in
95	/// the resolved range.
96	///
97	/// `_ctx` is unused (show has no user-config or `--log` consumer);
98	/// it's present for the uniform subcommand-layer signature.
99	pub fn show(_ctx: &Context, params: &ShowParams) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
100	    debug!("show: enter");
101	    let c = &params.common;
102	
103	    common::for_each_repo(&c.repos, &c.header, |workspace, repo| {
104	        let (ids, anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
105	            workspace,
106	            repo,
107	            &c.spec.rev,
108	            c.spec.desc_count,
109	            c.spec.anc_count,
110	        )?;
111	
112	        let mut first = true;
113	        for (i, commit_id) in ids.iter().enumerate() {
114	            if !first {
115	                info!("────────────────────────────────────────");
116	            }
117	            first = false;
118	
119	            let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
120	            show_one_commit(&commit, workspace, repo, params.files, i == anchor_index)?;
121	        }
122	        Ok(())
123	    })?;
124	    debug!("show: exit");
125	    Ok(())
126	}
127	
128	fn show_one_commit(
129	    commit: &Commit,
130	    workspace: &Workspace,
131	    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
132	    file_limit: FileLimit,
133	    is_primary: bool,
134	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
135	    // Author
136	    let author = commit.author();
137	    info!(
138	        "Author:    {} <{}> ({})",
139	        author.name,
140	        author.email,
141	        format_timestamp(&author.timestamp)
142	    );
143	
144	    // Committer
145	    let committer = commit.committer();
146	    info!(
147	        "Committer: {} <{}> ({})",
148	        committer.name,
149	        committer.email,
150	        format_timestamp(&committer.timestamp)
151	    );
152	
153	    // Ids
154	    let bookmarks = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, commit.id());
155	    let ids_line = common::format_commit_short(commit, &bookmarks);
156	    if is_primary {
157	        info!("Ids:       {}", common::bold(&ids_line));
158	    } else {
159	        info!("Ids:       {ids_line}");
160	    }
161	
162	    // Parents
163	    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
164	    for parent_id in commit.parent_ids() {
165	        if *parent_id == root_commit_id {
166	            continue;
167	        }
168	        let parent = repo.store().get_commit(parent_id)?;
169	        let bm = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, parent.id());
170	        info!("Parent:    {}", common::format_commit_short(&parent, &bm));
171	    }
172	
173	    // Children
174	    let children_ids =
175	        common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &format!("children({})", commit.id().hex()))?;
176	    for child_id in &children_ids {
177	        if *child_id == root_commit_id {
178	            continue;
179	        }
180	        let child = repo.store().get_commit(child_id)?;
181	        let bm = common::format_bookmarks_at(repo, child.id());
182	        info!("Child:     {}", common::format_commit_short(&child, &bm));
183	    }
184	
185	    // Branches
186	    let branches_str = format_branches(commit, workspace, repo)?;
187	    info!("Branches:  {branches_str}");
188	
189	    // Follows
190	    let follows = find_nearest_tag(commit, workspace, repo, true)?;
191	    info!("Follows:   {}", follows.as_deref().unwrap_or("")); // OK: obvious
192	
193	    // Precedes
194	    let precedes = find_nearest_tag(commit, workspace, repo, false)?;
195	    info!("Precedes:  {}", precedes.as_deref().unwrap_or("")); // OK: obvious
196	
197	    // Description (body only — title is on the Ids line)
198	    print_description(commit);
199	
200	    // Changed files
201	    if file_limit != FileLimit::None {
202	        print_diff(commit, repo, file_limit)?;
203	    }
204	
205	    Ok(())
206	}
207	
208	fn find_nearest_tag(
209	    commit: &Commit,
210	    workspace: &Workspace,
211	    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
212	    ancestor: bool,
213	) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
214	    let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
215	
216	    let revset_str = if ancestor {
217	        format!("heads(tags() & ::{})", commit_hex)
218	    } else {
219	        format!("roots(tags() & {}::)", commit_hex)
220	    };
221	
222	    let tag_ids = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
223	    if tag_ids.is_empty() {
224	        return Ok(None);
225	    }
226	
227	    let tag_id = &tag_ids[0];
228	    let view = repo.view();
229	    for (name, local_remote) in view.tags() {
230	        if local_remote.local_target.added_ids().any(|id| id == tag_id) {
231	            return Ok(Some(name.as_str().to_string()));
232	        }
233	    }
234	
235	    Ok(None)
236	}
237	
238	fn format_branches(
239	    commit: &Commit,
240	    workspace: &Workspace,
241	    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
242	) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
243	    let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
244	    let view = repo.view();
245	    let mut names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
246	
247	    for (name, local_remote) in view.bookmarks() {
248	        for bookmark_id in local_remote.local_target.added_ids() {
249	            let revset_str = format!("{} & ::{}", commit_hex, bookmark_id.hex());
250	            let results = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
251	            if !results.is_empty() {
252	                names.push(name.as_str().to_string());
253	                break;
254	            }
255	        }
256	
257	        for (remote_name, remote_ref) in &local_remote.remote_refs {
258	            if remote_name.as_str() == "git" {
259	                continue;
260	            }
261	            for bookmark_id in remote_ref.target.added_ids() {
262	                let revset_str = format!("{} & ::{}", commit_hex, bookmark_id.hex());
263	                let results = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
264	                if !results.is_empty() {
265	                    names.push(format!("{}@{}", name.as_str(), remote_name.as_str()));
266	                    break;
267	                }
268	            }
269	        }
270	    }
271	
272	    Ok(names.join(", "))
273	}
274	
275	fn format_timestamp(ts: &jj_lib::backend::Timestamp) -> String {
276	    let millis = ts.timestamp.0;
277	    let secs = millis / 1000;
278	    let tz_minutes = ts.tz_offset;
279	
280	    let dt = chrono::DateTime::from_timestamp(secs, 0)
281	        .unwrap_or_default() // OK: invalid timestamp → epoch for display
282	        .with_timezone(
283	            &chrono::FixedOffset::east_opt(tz_minutes * 60).unwrap_or(chrono::Utc.fix()), // OK: invalid tz → UTC for display
284	        );
285	
286	    dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S").to_string()
287	}
288	
289	fn print_description(commit: &Commit) {
290	    let desc = commit.description();
291	    let body: String = desc
292	        .lines()
293	        .skip(1)
294	        .skip_while(|l| l.is_empty())
295	        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
296	        .join("\n");
297	
298	    info!("Description:");
299	    for line in body.lines() {
300	        info!("    {line}");
301	    }
302	}
303	
304	fn print_diff(
305	    commit: &Commit,
306	    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
307	    file_limit: FileLimit,
308	) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
309	    let parent_tree = commit.parent_tree(repo.as_ref()).block_on()?;
310	    let commit_tree = commit.tree();
311	
312	    let diff_iter = TreeDiffIterator::new(&parent_tree, &commit_tree, &EverythingMatcher);
313	
314	    let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
315	    for entry in diff_iter {
316	        let diff = entry.values?;
317	        let before = diff.before.as_resolved().cloned().flatten();
318	        let after = diff.after.as_resolved().cloned().flatten();
319	
320	        if matches!(&before, Some(TreeValue::Tree(_))) || matches!(&after, Some(TreeValue::Tree(_)))
321	        {
322	            continue;
323	        }
324	
325	        let path_str = entry.path.as_internal_file_string().to_string();
326	
327	        let line = match (before, after) {
328	            (None, Some(value)) => {
329	                format!("    Added {} {path_str}", file_type_str(&value))
330	            }
331	            (Some(_), None) => {
332	                format!(
333	                    "    Removed {} {path_str}",
334	                    file_type_str_from_merged(&diff.before),
335	                )
336	            }
337	            (Some(_), Some(value)) => {
338	                format!("    Modified {} {path_str}", file_type_str(&value))
339	            }
340	            (None, None) => continue,
341	        };
342	        lines.push(line);
343	    }
344	
345	    let total = lines.len();
346	    let cap = match file_limit {
347	        FileLimit::All | FileLimit::None => 0,
348	        FileLimit::Cap(n) => n,
349	    };
350	    let show_all = cap == 0 || total <= cap;
351	
352	    if show_all {
353	        info!("Changed files: {total}");
354	        for line in &lines {
355	            info!("{line}");
356	        }
357	    } else {
358	        info!("Changed files: {total}, showing first {cap}");
359	        for line in &lines[..cap] {
360	            info!("{line}");
361	        }
362	    }
363	
364	    Ok(())
365	}
366	
367	fn file_type_str(value: &TreeValue) -> &'static str {
368	    match value {
369	        TreeValue::File {
370	            executable: false, ..
371	        } => "regular file",
372	        TreeValue::File {
373	            executable: true, ..
374	        } => "executable file",
375	        TreeValue::Symlink(_) => "symlink",
376	        TreeValue::Tree(_) => "tree",
377	        TreeValue::GitSubmodule(_) => "git submodule",
378	    }
379	}
380	
381	fn file_type_str_from_merged(merged: &jj_lib::merge::Merge<Option<TreeValue>>) -> &'static str {
382	    if let Some(Some(value)) = merged.as_resolved() {
383	        file_type_str(value)
384	    } else {
385	        "file"
386	    }
387	}
388	
389	#[cfg(test)]
390	mod tests {
391	    use std::path::PathBuf;
392	
393	    use clap::Parser;
394	
395	    use crate::{Cli, Commands};
396	
397	    fn parse(args: &[&str]) -> super::ShowArgs {
398	        let cli = Cli::try_parse_from(args).unwrap();
399	        match cli.command {
400	            Commands::Show(a) => a,
401	            _ => panic!("expected Show"),
402	        }
403	    }
404	
405	    #[test]
406	    fn defaults() {
407	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show"]);
408	        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, "@");
409	        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, None);
410	        assert_eq!(args.common.scope, None);
411	        assert_eq!(args.files, "50");
412	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, None);
413	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, None);
414	        assert_eq!(args.common.limit, None);
415	    }
416	
417	    #[test]
418	    fn with_revision() {
419	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-r", "@-"]);
420	        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, "@-");
421	    }
422	
423	    #[test]
424	    fn with_repo() {
425	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-R", ".claude"]);
426	        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, Some(PathBuf::from(".claude")));
427	    }
428	
429	    #[test]
430	    fn with_scope_code() {
431	        use crate::scope::{Scope, Side};
432	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-s", "code"]);
433	        assert_eq!(args.common.scope, Some(Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code])));
434	    }
435	
436	    #[test]
437	    fn positional_rev() {
438	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "@-"]);
439	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, Some("@-".to_string()));
440	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, None);
441	    }
442	
443	    #[test]
444	    fn positional_rev_and_count() {
445	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "@..", "3"]);
446	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_rev, Some("@..".to_string()));
447	        assert_eq!(args.common.pos_count, Some(3));
448	    }
449	
450	    #[test]
451	    fn with_file_limit_flag() {
452	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-f", "0"]);
453	        assert_eq!(args.files, "0");
454	    }
455	
456	    #[test]
457	    fn with_file_limit_all() {
458	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-f", "all"]);
459	        assert_eq!(args.files, "all");
460	    }
461	
462	    #[test]
463	    fn with_commit_limit() {
464	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-n", "5"]);
465	        assert_eq!(args.common.limit, Some(5));
466	    }
467	
468	    #[test]
469	    fn all_opts() {
470	        let args = parse(&[
471	            "vc-x1", "show", "-r", "@--", "-R", "/tmp", "-f", "100", "-n", "3",
472	        ]);
473	        assert_eq!(args.common.revision, "@--");
474	        assert_eq!(args.common.repo, Some(PathBuf::from("/tmp")));
475	        assert_eq!(args.files, "100");
476	        assert_eq!(args.common.limit, Some(3));
477	    }
478	
479	    #[test]
480	    fn params_from_args_defaults() {
481	        // ShowParams::try_from goes through the binary-edge resolution
482	        // and parses --files into a FileLimit. Default "50" → Cap(50).
483	        use super::{FileLimit, ShowParams};
484	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show"]);
485	        let params = ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap();
486	        assert_eq!(params.common.repos, vec![PathBuf::from(".")]);
487	        assert_eq!(params.common.spec.rev, "@");
488	        assert_eq!(params.files, FileLimit::Cap(50));
489	    }
490	
491	    #[test]
492	    fn params_from_args_files_variants() {
493	        use super::{FileLimit, ShowParams};
494	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-f", "0"]);
495	        assert_eq!(ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap().files, FileLimit::None);
496	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-f", "all"]);
497	        assert_eq!(ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap().files, FileLimit::All);
498	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-f", "5"]);
499	        assert_eq!(
500	            ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap().files,
501	            FileLimit::Cap(5)
502	        );
503	    }
504	
505	    #[test]
506	    fn params_from_args_files_invalid() {
507	        // FileLimit::parse error surfaces through ShowParams::try_from.
508	        use super::ShowParams;
509	        let args = parse(&["vc-x1", "show", "-f", "bogus"]);
510	        let err = ShowParams::try_from(&args).unwrap_err();
511	        assert!(err.contains("invalid file limit"), "got: {err}");
512	    }
513	}

2	//!
3	//! - `--scope=code,bot` (default): dual-repo layout — clones code,
4	//!   derives bot source (`<source>.claude`), clones bot into
5	//!   `<target>/.claude`, creates the Claude Code symlink. Both
6	//!   sides must succeed.
7	//! - `--scope=por`: single repo into `<target>`. No `.claude/`,
8	//!   no symlink.
9	//!
10	//! TARGET shapes (all routed through `parse_target`): URL,
11	//! `owner/name` shorthand, or a local path (`./X`, `/X`, `~/X`,
12	//! `.`, `..`). Path-form is symmetric with `git clone /local/bare.git`
13	//! — useful for fixtures and CI scratch dirs.
14	//!

1	//! The `sync` subcommand: fetch + classify + (optionally) rebase /
2	//! fast-forward a workspace's repos against their remotes.
3	//!
4	//! Default is `--check` (fetch + report, error if any repo needs
5	//! action); `--no-check` applies. On any failure every repo is
6	//! reverted to its pre-sync op via `jj op restore`.
7	//!
8	//! `current_op_id` / `op_restore` are `pub(crate)` so `push` reuses
9	//! the same snapshot-and-restore pattern.
10

20	
21	/// Fetch and sync a set of repos to their remotes.
22	///
23	/// Default is `--check`: fetch + classify + report, **error** if any
24	/// repo is `behind` or `diverged`. Pass `--no-check` to actually
25	/// rebase / fast-forward. On any failure the starting state of every
26	/// repo is restored via `jj op restore`.
27	///
28	/// Scripts and automation should pass `--check` or `--no-check`
29	/// explicitly rather than relying on the default — defaults can shift,
30	/// explicit flags lock in the contract.

85	/// Squash, set bookmark, and/or push a jj repo.
86	///
87	/// Designed for the bot to atomically finalize its session repo:
88	/// --detach exits immediately, --delay waits for trailing writes,
89	/// --squash folds them in, --bookmark + --push sends it upstream.
90	/// Every flag is opt-in. See README.md for details.
91	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
92	pub struct FinalizeArgs {
93	    /// Path to jj repo
94	    #[arg(long, default_value = ".")]
95	    pub repo: PathBuf,
96	
97	    #[command(flatten)]
98	    pub squash: SquashOption,
99	
100	    /// Seconds to wait before squashing
101	    #[arg(long, default_value_t = 10.0)]
102	    pub delay: f64,
103	
104	    /// Existing bookmark to push to remote
105	    #[arg(long, value_name = "BOOKMARK")]
106	    pub push: Option<String>,
107	
108	    /// Detach and run in the background

110	    pub detach: bool,
111	
112	    /// The above `pub detach` is true when `--detach` is passed
113	    /// to the `finalize` subcommand. The app then `fork`s and
114	    /// spawns `vc-x1 finalize --exec …`, replacing `--detach`
115	    /// with `--exec`. The child sees `exec: true` and is routed

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/notes/chores-10.md",
  "old_string": "- `notes/todo.md`: ladder updated — 0.50.0-4 →\n  `(done)`; add `0.50.0-5 port 8 subcommands (current)`\n  and `0.50.0-6 port finalize` entry.\n\n# References",
  "new_string": "- `notes/todo.md`: ladder updated — 0.50.0-4 →\n  `(done)`; add `0.50.0-5 port 8 subcommands (current)`\n  and `0.50.0-6 port finalize` entry.\n\n## refactor: finalize → SubcommandRunner (0.50.0-6)\n\nLast per-command port: `finalize` joins the other 11\narms on `SubcommandRunner::dispatch`. The\n`is_detached_exec` slot on the trait (default `false`,\nunused since `-1`) finally gets its implementor —\n`FinalizeArgs::is_detached_exec(params)` returns\n`params.exec`, which the trait's default `dispatch`\nthreads into `crate::sb_ide` so the detached\n`finalize --exec` child stays silent.\n\n`FinalizeParams` already carried `exec: bool` from\nthe existing `TryFrom` impl — no new field needed.\nThe trait override reads it directly.\n\nWhat lands in this commit:\n\n- The Finalize arm in `main.rs` collapses to one line.\n  Every match arm is now `args.dispatch(&ctx)`.\n- The `run_command` helper in `main.rs` becomes dead\n  (no remaining caller) and is deleted. Clippy\n  `-D warnings` would block the cycle otherwise; the\n  deletion is the natural consequence of the last\n  port.\n\nWhat does **not** land — deferred to `-K`:\n\n- The top-level `let is_detached_exec = match\n  cli.command { Commands::Finalize(ref f) => f.exec,\n  _ => false };` block in `main.rs` stays. It gates\n  the `bm_track` enter/exit pair which lives outside\n  `dispatch`. Moving those calls inside the trait\n  (so `main` no longer needs the peek) is `-K`'s job.\n\nAfter this commit `main.rs`'s exec_code match is\ntwelve `args.dispatch(&ctx)` lines and nothing else.\n\n- `Cargo.toml`: `0.50.0-5` → `0.50.0-6`.\n- `src/finalize.rs`: `SubcommandRunner for FinalizeArgs`\n  impl added; `is_detached_exec(params)` override\n  returns `params.exec`.\n- `src/main.rs`: `Finalize` arm collapsed to\n  `args.dispatch(&ctx)`; `fn run_command` deleted\n  (now unused).\n- `notes/chores-10.md`: backfilled `Commits: [[20]]`\n  on the 0.50.0-5 section.\n- `notes/todo.md`: 0.50.0-5 → `(done)`; 0.50.0-6\n  marked `(current)`.\n\n# References"
}

Finalize is ported — every match arm in `main.rs` is now `args.dispatch(&ctx)`. Dead `run_command` helper deleted. Top-level `is_detached_exec` match stays (gates `bm_track`) — K's job.

Proposed commit title and body:

```
refactor: finalize → SubcommandRunner (0.50.0-6)
```

```
Last per-command port: `finalize` joins the other 11
arms on `SubcommandRunner::dispatch`. The
`is_detached_exec` slot on the trait (default `false`,
unused since `-1`) finally gets its implementor —
`FinalizeArgs::is_detached_exec(params)` returns
`params.exec`, which the trait's default `dispatch`
threads into `crate::sb_ide` so the detached
`finalize --exec` child stays silent.

After this commit `main.rs`'s exec_code match is
twelve `args.dispatch(&ctx)` lines and nothing else.
The top-level `is_detached_exec` match in `main`
stays (gates `bm_track` enter/exit, which lives
outside `dispatch`); moving it inside the trait is
`-K`'s job. See `notes/chores-10.md > refactor:
finalize → SubcommandRunner (0.50.0-6)` for the full
record.

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0-5 → 0.50.0-6.
- src/finalize.rs: `SubcommandRunner for FinalizeArgs`
  impl added.
- src/main.rs: `Finalize` arm collapsed; `fn
  run_command` deleted (no remaining caller).
- notes/chores-10.md: backfilled `Commits: [[20]]`
  on the 0.50.0-5 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.50.0-5 → (done).
```

245	/// refinement would remove the steady-state noise without losing
246	/// detection value (see `notes/todo.md`).
247	///
248	/// Walks up from cwd to locate the workspace root (the directory
249	/// whose `.vc-config.toml` has `path = "/"`), then probes `<root>`
250	/// and `<root>/.claude`. Same labeling whether the user runs from
251	/// the app root, from `.claude`, or from any subdir.
252	fn bm_track(phase: &str, command_name: &str) {
253	    let header = format!("bm-track {phase} vc-x1 {command_name}");
254	    let root = match common::find_workspace_root() {
255	        Some(r) => r,
256	        None => {
257	            log::info!("{header}: no-workspace");
258	            return;
259	        }
260	    };
261	    let repos: [(std::path::PathBuf, &str); 2] =
262	        [(root.clone(), "app"), (root.join(".claude"), ".claude")];
263	    let mut parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
264	    for (repo, label) in repos {
265	        if !repo.join(".jj").exists() {
266	            parts.push(format!("{label}(main)=no-jj"));
267	            continue;
268	        }
269	        match bm_track_one(&repo, "main", "origin") {
270	            Ok(true) => parts.push(format!("{label}(main)=tracked")),
271	            Ok(false) => parts.push(format!("{label}(main)=NOT_TRACKED")),
272	            Err(e) => parts.push(format!(
273	                "{label}(main)=err({})",
274	                e.lines().next().unwrap_or("")

```
1	//! `SubcommandRunner` trait
2	//!
3	//! The purpose is to simplify invoking a subcommand so `main`
4	//! can invoke any subcommand with one line:
5	//!
6	//! `Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),`
7	//!
8	//! Instead of the ~11 lines that the default `dispatch` below
9	//! now encapsulates.
10	//!
11	//! Implementors provide:
12	//!
13	//! - [`SubcommandRunner::to_params`] — build the clap-free
14	//!   `Params` from the clap `Args` (covers both `From` /
15	//!   `TryFrom` shapes uniformly via `Result<_, String>`).
16	//! - [`SubcommandRunner::run`] — the subcommand body.
17	//!
18	//! The default [`SubcommandRunner::dispatch`] builds `Params`
19	//! via `to_params`, runs via `run`, and maps the result to
20	//! `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via `error!` and return
21	//! `ExitCode::FAILURE`. `Context` is loaded once in `main` and
22	//! passed in by reference so a single `Context` is shared across
23	//! the (one) match arm that actually runs.
24	//!
25	//! ## See also
26	//!
27	//! - The wider CLI-args / subcommand-layer split this trait
28	//!   formalizes: [`ARCHITECTURE.md > args → Context + Params`][arch].
29	//! - Cycle design, ladder, evaluation gate:
30	//!   [`chores-10.md > 0.50.0-0`][open].
31	//! - Worked example, trait-shape decisions, naming:
32	//!   [`chores-10.md > 0.50.0-1`][port].
33	//! - First implementor: [`crate::chid`].
34	//!
35	//! [arch]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/ARCHITECTURE.md#args--context--params
36	//! [open]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/notes/chores-10.md#chore-open-subcommand-trait-sweep-0500-0
37	//! [port]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/notes/chores-10.md#refactor-subcommandrunner-trait--chid-0500-1
38	
39	use std::process::ExitCode;
40	
41	use log::error;
42	
43	use crate::context::Context;
44	
45	/// Trait implemented by every subcommand's clap `Args` type so
46	/// `main.rs` can dispatch via a single `args.dispatch(&ctx)`
47	/// call.
48	pub trait SubcommandRunner {
49	    /// The clap-free `Params` struct the subcommand body
50	    /// consumes.
51	    type Params;
52	
53	    /// Build `Params` from `Args`. `Result<_, String>` absorbs
54	    /// both `From` (return `Ok(...)`) and `TryFrom` (forward
55	    /// the error) shapes uniformly.
56	    fn to_params(&self) -> Result<Self::Params, String>;
57	
58	    /// Run the subcommand body.
59	    fn run(ctx: &Context, params: &Self::Params) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>>;
60	
61	    /// Whether to suppress the leading `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner.
62	    /// Default `false`; commands whose `Params` carry a
63	    /// banner-suppression flag (e.g. `chid` / `desc` / `list` /
64	    /// `show` under `-L`) override to read it.
65	    fn suppress_banner(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
66	        false
67	    }
68	
69	    /// Whether this invocation is the detached `finalize --exec`
70	    /// child. Default `false`; `finalize` (when ported) overrides
71	    /// to read it from its `Params`.
72	    fn is_detached_exec(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
73	        false
74	    }
75	
76	    /// Default dispatch: build `Params` via `to_params`, emit
77	    /// session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`], run via `run`, and
78	    /// map the result to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via
79	    /// `error!` and return `ExitCode::FAILURE`.
80	    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {
81	        let params = match self.to_params() {
82	            Ok(p) => p,
83	            Err(e) => {
84	                error!("{e}");
85	                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
86	            }
87	        };
88	        crate::sb_ide(
89	            Self::suppress_banner(&params),
90	            Self::is_detached_exec(&params),
91	        );
92	        match Self::run(ctx, &params) {
93	            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
94	            Err(e) => {
95	                error!("{e}");
96	                ExitCode::FAILURE
97	            }
98	        }
99	    }
100	}
101	
```

  "old_string": "    /// Whether this invocation is the detached `finalize --exec`\n    /// child. Default `false`; `finalize` (when ported) overrides\n    /// to read it from its `Params`.\n    fn is_detached_exec(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {\n        false\n    }\n\n    /// Default dispatch: build `Params` via `to_params`, emit\n    /// session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`], run via `run`, and\n    /// map the result to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via\n    /// `error!` and return `ExitCode::FAILURE`.\n    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {\n        let params = match self.to_params() {\n            Ok(p) => p,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                return ExitCode::FAILURE;\n            }\n        };\n        crate::sb_ide(\n            Self::suppress_banner(&params),\n            Self::is_detached_exec(&params),\n        );\n        match Self::run(ctx, &params) {\n            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                ExitCode::FAILURE\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "    /// Whether this invocation is the detached `finalize --exec`\n    /// child. Default `false`; `finalize` overrides to read it\n    /// from its `Params`. Consumed by `dispatch` for both the\n    /// banner suppression (via `crate::sb_ide`) and the\n    /// `bm_track` enter/exit gating (the detached child shouldn't\n    /// emit user-facing bookmark-tracking lines).\n    fn is_detached_exec(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {\n        false\n    }\n\n    /// Default dispatch: build `Params` via `to_params`, emit\n    /// session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`], bracket the run with\n    /// `crate::bm_track` enter/exit (skipped when\n    /// `is_detached_exec`), execute via `run`, and map the result\n    /// to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via `error!` and\n    /// return `ExitCode::FAILURE`.\n    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {\n        let params = match self.to_params() {\n            Ok(p) => p,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                return ExitCode::FAILURE;\n            }\n        };\n        let is_detached = Self::is_detached_exec(&params);\n        crate::sb_ide(Self::suppress_banner(&params), is_detached);\n\n        // Command name is the first positional after the binary;\n        // clap has already validated it by the time we reach\n        // dispatch (top-level parse errors exit earlier).\n        let command_name = std::env::args().nth(1).unwrap_or_else(|| \"?\".to_string()); // OK: default when somehow invoked without a subcommand\n\n        if !is_detached {\n            crate::bm_track(\"enter\", &command_name);\n        }\n        let exit_code = match Self::run(ctx, &params) {\n            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                ExitCode::FAILURE\n            }\n        };\n        if !is_detached {\n            crate::bm_track(\"exit \", &command_name);\n        }\n        exit_code\n    }\n}"
}

> AGENT

Work complete. `main`'s body is now just `Context::load` plus a thin match dispatch. `bm_track` enter/exit gates live in `SubcommandRunner::dispatch` and read `is_detached_exec` off the trait.

Proposed commit title and body:

```
refactor: move bm_track into dispatch (0.50.0-7)
```

```
Cycle cleanup (formerly the `-K` placeholder). With
every subcommand on `SubcommandRunner::dispatch`, the
top-level `is_detached_exec` match and the `bm_track`
enter/exit gates that consumed it no longer need to
live in `main`. Move them inside `dispatch`, where
they read the trait's `is_detached_exec(&params)`
directly.

`main.rs`'s `fn main` collapses to a `Context::load`
plus a thin twelve-arm dispatch match — each arm a
single `args.dispatch(&ctx)` line, and `main` does no
other work. The match itself stays for compile-time
exhaustiveness on `Commands`. See
`notes/chores-10.md > refactor: move bm_track into
dispatch (0.50.0-7)` for the full record.

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0-6 → 0.50.0-7.
- src/main.rs: top-level `is_detached_exec` match,
  `command_name` line, and `bm_track` enter/exit
  gates deleted; `fn bm_track` made `pub`.
- src/subcommand.rs: `dispatch` reads
  `is_detached_exec` once and threads it through
  `sb_ide` plus the new `bm_track` enter/exit
  brackets.
- notes/chores-10.md: backfilled `Commits: [[21]]`
  on the 0.50.0-6 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.50.0-6 → (done); rename `K` line
  to `0.50.0-7`.
```

OK to push?

  "old_string": "- `notes/todo.md`: 0.50.0-6 → `(done)`; rename\n  `0.50.0-K …` line to `0.50.0-7 main.rs dispatch\n  rework (current)`.\n\n# References",
  "new_string": "- `notes/todo.md`: 0.50.0-6 → `(done)`; rename\n  `0.50.0-K …` line to `0.50.0-7 main.rs dispatch\n  rework (current)`.\n\n## chore: close Subcommand trait sweep (0.50.0)\n\nCycle close-out. All 12 subcommands now run through\n`SubcommandRunner::dispatch`; `main.rs`'s `fn main`\nis `Context::load` + a 12-arm dispatch match and\nnothing else. The per-arm boilerplate the cycle set\nout to remove (`Context::load` + `try_from` +\n`run_command`, ~12 lines × 12 arms) is gone; each arm\nis a single `args.dispatch(&ctx)` line. The\n`Commands` enum stays the dispatch source of truth\n(compile-time exhaustiveness preserved), so the\n`linkme` / `inventory` follow-up items in `## Todo`\nremain queued as higher-leverage alternatives if the\nper-arm cost ever feels burdensome again.\n\n### As-built ladder\n\n- 0.50.0-0 plan + version bump + chores section +\n  todo ladder + linkme/inventory todos\n- 0.50.0-1 add `subcommand.rs` (`SubcommandRunner`\n  trait) + port `chid` (worked example, three\n  material design calls landed: trait rename\n  `Subcommand` → `SubcommandRunner` to dodge\n  `clap::Subcommand` collision; session-chrome\n  extracted as `pub fn sb_ide`; peek methods read\n  from `Params`)\n- 0.50.0-1.1 dicom-rs gotchas → todo (inter-substep\n  tidy)\n- 0.50.0-1.2 --exec doc + matches! → match\n  (inter-substep tidy)\n- 0.50.0-2 hoist `Context::load` to a single site in\n  `main`; change `dispatch` to take `&Context`\n- 0.50.0-3 port `desc`\n- 0.50.0-4 port `list`\n- 0.50.0-5 bulk port: `show` + 7 `From` commands\n  (`validate_desc`, `fix_desc`, `clone`, `init`,\n  `symlink`, `sync`, `push`); top-level\n  `suppress_banner` match deleted\n- 0.50.0-6 port `finalize` (`is_detached_exec`\n  override wires `params.exec` through the trait);\n  `fn run_command` deleted from `main`\n- 0.50.0-7 move `bm_track` enter/exit into\n  `dispatch`; delete the top-level\n  `is_detached_exec` match in `main` (was the `-K`\n  placeholder)\n\n### Outcome\n\nThe trait approach landed cleanly — none of the\nper-substep evaluation gates fired the \"modify shape\nsignificantly\" or \"abandon\" outcomes the open\nsection reserved. The shape settled at `-1` (with the\nthree design calls noted above) held for the\nremaining 10 ports without revision. `linkme` /\n`inventory` would have gone further (eliminate the\n`Commands` enum entirely), but at compile-time\nexhaustiveness and macro-magic costs; they remain\nqueued in `## Todo` as separate items.\n\n- `Cargo.toml`: `0.50.0-7` → `0.50.0` (suffix\n  dropped — cycle close marker).\n- `notes/chores-10.md`: backfilled `Commits: [[22]]`\n  on the 0.50.0-7 section; new close-out section\n  with the as-built ladder + outcome notes.\n- `notes/todo.md`: deleted the In Progress ladder\n  block; added `Subcommand trait sweep — 12\n  subcommands ported via SubcommandRunner trait,\n  main.rs collapsed to Context::load + thin dispatch\n  (0.50.0)` to `## Done` with `[[37]]` ref.\n\n# References"
}

Proposed commit title and body:

```
chore: close Subcommand trait sweep (0.50.0)

```
Cycle close-out. All 12 subcommands now run through
`SubcommandRunner::dispatch`; `main.rs`'s `fn main`
is `Context::load` + a 12-arm dispatch match and
nothing else. The per-arm boilerplate the cycle set
out to remove (`Context::load` + `try_from` +
`run_command`, ~12 lines × 12 arms) is gone; each arm
is a single `args.dispatch(&ctx)` line.

The trait approach landed cleanly — none of the
per-substep evaluation gates fired the "modify shape
significantly" or "abandon" outcomes the open
section reserved. Shape settled at `-1` and held for
the remaining 10 ports. `linkme` / `inventory` remain
queued in `## Todo` as higher-leverage alternatives.

See `notes/chores-10.md > chore: close Subcommand
trait sweep (0.50.0)` for the as-built ladder and
outcome notes.

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0-7 → 0.50.0 (suffix dropped).
- notes/chores-10.md: backfilled `Commits: [[22]]`
  on the 0.50.0-7 section; new close-out section.
- notes/todo.md: In Progress ladder deleted; added
  Done entry with `[[37]]` ref.
```
