200	        Stages: preflight (fmt/clippy/test) → review (approve diff)\n\
201	        → message ($EDITOR / --title+--body, approve text) →\n\
202	        commit-app → commit-claude (skipped if clean) → bookmark-both\n\
203	        → push-app → finalize-claude. Failures in commit-app /\n\
204	        commit-claude / bookmark-both roll both repos back via\n\
205	        `jj op restore` to the snapshot recorded before commit-app.\n\
206	        After push-app succeeds the remote boundary is crossed and\n\
207	        recovery is forward-only.\n\n\
208	        Non-interactive use: pass both --title and --body plus --yes\n\
209	        to skip the review gate. Saved state carries title/body\n\
210	        across resumes so only the first invocation needs them."
211	    )]
212	    Push(push::PushArgs),
213	}
214	
215	/// Session chrome: emit the leading banner (unless suppressed) and
216	/// surface any failures left over from the previous run, gated on
217	/// "this isn't the detached `finalize --exec` re-entry".
218	///
219	/// Called from the trait's default `dispatch` (for ported
220	/// subcommands, reading the bools from each command's `Params`)
221	/// and from each unported arm in `fn main()` (reading the bools
222	/// from the per-arm peek match). When all arms are ported, the
223	/// `main`-side callers disappear; only the trait callers remain.
224	pub fn sb_ide(suppress_banner: bool, is_detached_exec: bool) {
225	    if !is_detached_exec {
226	        if !suppress_banner {
227	            // Banner on every normal run, mirroring what `--help`
228	            // shows at the top.
229	            log::info!("{BANNER}");
230	        }
231	        finalize::surface_previous_failures();
232	    }
233	}
234	
235	/// Permanent sanity check for the `main`-bookmark tracking state
236	/// in both repos of the dual-repo workspace. Prints one line on
237	/// entry and one on exit of every command. If entry and exit
238	/// differ, the executing command is the culprit; if entry differs
239	/// from the previous command's exit, something *between*
240	/// invocations broke it. Originally added in 0.37.2 as a temporary
241	/// diagnostic; promoted to permanent in 0.37.4 after the user
242	/// reported "happens more than once" — the signal is worth keeping
243	/// in place until confidence in its correctness is well-established
244	/// across varied use, at which point a "silent when clean"

> AGENT

Moving `bm_track` to `debug!` works — `-v` runs see it, default runs don't, and the detached child (no `-v`) wouldn't print anyway, so the `if !is_detached` gates around `bm_track` become dead and can go. That's a clean step.

But it doesn't yet kill `is_detached_exec` on the trait — `sb_ide` still consumes it for two things (suppress the `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner, and gate `finalize::surface_previous_failures`). Both have meaningful reasons to skip in the detached child (chatter in its log; failures meant for next interactive run shouldn't get consumed by the child).

So shape suggestion for 0.51.0 (multi-step, chores-11):

- **0.51.0-0** plan + version bump + chores-11.md + design subsection on the trio (`sb_ide` / `is_detached_exec` / `suppress_banner`)
- **0.51.0-1** `bm_track` → `debug!`, drop the gates around it in `dispatch`
- **0.51.0-2..N** eliminate `sb_ide` — likely banner-on-every-run goes away (clap's `--help` pre_help already shows it), `surface_previous_failures` moves to finalize-side
- **0.51.0-3..N** drop `is_detached_exec` from trait, then `suppress_banner` from trait
- **0.51.0** close-out

Want me to open the cycle at 0.51.0-0 with chores-11 + this plan?

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/notes/chores-11.md",
  "content": "# Chores-11\n\nContinuation of `chores-10.md` (which is closed at `0.50.0` —\nthe Subcommand trait sweep). This file covers the `0.51.0`\ncycle onward. Reference numbering is file-local — see\n[`README.md`](README.md#reference-numbering); chores-11\nstarts at `[1]`.\n\n## chore: open sb_ide elimination (0.51.0-0)\n\nMulti-step. The `0.50.0` cycle landed every subcommand on\n`SubcommandRunner::dispatch`, with two trait peek methods\n(`suppress_banner` / `is_detached_exec`) and one free function\n(`sb_ide`) carrying the per-run \"session chrome\" — the\n`vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner, `finalize::surface_previous_failures`,\nand the bm_track enter/exit pair. This cycle eliminates all\nthree by relocating each behavior to where it actually\nbelongs:\n\n- The `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner already lands on `--help` via\n  clap's `before_help`; printing it again at the top of every\n  normal run is duplicate chatter the user can recover from\n  `--version`. Drop the on-every-run banner.\n- `finalize::surface_previous_failures` is a finalize-side\n  concern; moving its trigger into `finalize` itself (rather\n  than into a generic dispatch hook) cleans up the gate.\n- `bm_track` enter/exit is a diagnostic; demoting to `debug!`\n  matches the \"permanent sanity check, but quiet by default\"\n  intent already recorded in its module doc.\n\nAfter these relocations the trait's `suppress_banner` /\n`is_detached_exec` methods have no remaining consumers, and\n`sb_ide` has no body left to keep — all three disappear.\nPlan-only opener; the work lands in the substeps below.\n\n### What goes away — and the reasoning\n\n- **`bm_track` info-level emission.** Currently emits\n  `bm-track enter vc-x1 X: app(main)=tracked, …` on every\n  run. The module doc already notes \"a future refinement\n  would remove the steady-state noise without losing\n  detection value\" — `debug!` accomplishes exactly that.\n  Detection value is preserved (run with `-v` when\n  investigating); steady-state output gets cleaner.\n- **`sb_ide`'s banner-on-every-run.** `clap::Command::before_help`\n  already shows the banner on `--help`. The on-every-run\n  emission is duplicate; users who want it explicitly have\n  `vc-x1 -V`. Removing it shrinks the default output by one\n  line per command.\n- **`finalize::surface_previous_failures` gated in `sb_ide`.**\n  This is finalize machinery — it reads `.vc-x1/finalize-fail-*`\n  markers and surfaces them. The \"don't surface from the\n  detached child\" gate is finalize's concern, not the trait's;\n  moving the trigger into `finalize` itself lets the gate live\n  where the marker logic lives.\n- **`SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec`.** With `bm_track`\n  at `debug!` and `surface_previous_failures` relocated, no\n  remaining consumer needs the trait's detached-exec peek.\n  Drop the method; `finalize::is_detached_exec` (or\n  equivalent) is a finalize-private concern.\n- **`SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner`.** With the banner\n  gone from `sb_ide`, the `-L` flag still suppresses bookmark\n  labels in the listing subcommands' output (chid/desc/list/\n  show via `CommonParams::header`) — that already lives in\n  the op layer, not the chrome layer. The trait peek has no\n  remaining consumer; drop it.\n\n### Ladder\n\n- 0.51.0-0 plan + version bump + this section + todo\n  ladder (current)\n- 0.51.0-1 `bm_track` → `debug!`; drop the bm_track gates\n  in `SubcommandRunner::dispatch`\n- 0.51.0-2..N eliminate `sb_ide`:\n  - Drop banner-on-every-run from `sb_ide`\n  - Move `finalize::surface_previous_failures` trigger\n    into `finalize` itself\n  - Delete `sb_ide` once both bodies have moved\n- 0.51.0-K drop `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec` (no\n  remaining consumer) and `SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner`\n  (no remaining consumer); drop `suppress_banner` fields\n  from the four `Params` types that carry them\n  (`ChidParams`, `DescParams`, `ListParams`, `ShowParams`).\n- 0.51.0 close-out\n\n### Per-step evaluation\n\nEffectiveness is evaluated after each substep. Possible\noutcomes at any step boundary:\n\n- Continue as planned.\n- Significantly modify the shape (e.g. keep the banner\n  somewhere else; preserve `is_detached_exec` for a use\n  case discovered mid-cycle) — recorded in a new chores\n  subsection at the next step.\n- Abandon: revert the cycle to a non-eliminated baseline,\n  closing as a no-op with an `### Outcome` note capturing\n  what didn't fit.\n\n# References\n"
}

```
Multi-step cycle opener. The 0.50.0 cycle landed every
subcommand on `SubcommandRunner::dispatch` with two
trait peek methods (`suppress_banner` /
`is_detached_exec`) and one free function (`sb_ide`)
carrying per-run "session chrome" — the banner,
`finalize::surface_previous_failures`, and `bm_track`
enter/exit. This cycle eliminates all three by
relocating each behavior where it actually belongs:
banner already lives on `--help` (drop the on-every-run
emission), `surface_previous_failures` moves into
finalize, `bm_track` → `debug!`. After the relocations
the trait peeks have no remaining consumers and
`sb_ide` has no body left to keep.

Plan-only commit; the work lands across 0.51.0-N
substeps. See `notes/chores-11.md > chore: open
sb_ide elimination (0.51.0-0)` for the full design +
ladder.

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0 → 0.51.0-0.
- notes/chores-11.md: new file with the cycle opener.
- notes/chores-10.md: backfilled `Commits: [[23]]` on
  the 0.50.0 close-out section.
- notes/todo.md: new In Progress ladder for the
  cycle.
```

- `done.md` uses single-bracket `[N]` citations historically (markdown links — brackets don't render). Sticking with that style for the new entries avoids mixed-style churn in the archive file. The newer `[[N]]` convention is for live notes where you want brackets visible — appropriate for `todo.md` / `chores-*.md`, less so for an archive.
- Natural fit for this commit (`0.51.0-0`) — it's plan-only, no code changes, so todo.md / done.md hygiene rides along cleanly. The commit body / chores section gain one bullet noting the move.

> AGENT

Done.md migration folded in. Updated commit body:

```
chore: open sb_ide elimination (0.51.0-0)
```

```
Multi-step cycle opener. The 0.50.0 cycle landed every
subcommand on `SubcommandRunner::dispatch` with two
trait peek methods (`suppress_banner` /
`is_detached_exec`) and one free function (`sb_ide`)
carrying per-run "session chrome" — the banner,
`finalize::surface_previous_failures`, and `bm_track`
enter/exit. This cycle eliminates all three by
relocating each behavior where it actually belongs:
banner already lives on `--help` (drop the on-every-run
emission), `surface_previous_failures` moves into
finalize, `bm_track` → `debug!`. After the relocations
the trait peeks have no remaining consumers and
`sb_ide` has no body left to keep.

Plan-only commit; the work lands across 0.51.0-N
substeps. See `notes/chores-11.md > chore: open
sb_ide elimination (0.51.0-0)` for the full design +
ladder. Done.md hygiene rides along — the 11 entries
spanning 0.44.0 → 0.50.0 migrate from todo.md ## Done
to done.md.

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0 → 0.51.0-0.
- notes/chores-11.md: new file with the cycle opener.
- notes/chores-10.md: backfilled `Commits: [[23]]` on
  the 0.50.0 close-out section.
- notes/todo.md: new In Progress ladder for the
  cycle; ## Done entries (0.44.0–0.50.0) and their
  refs ([1], [2], [3], [30]–[37]) deleted with a
  one-line migration note.
- notes/done.md: 11 entries appended (renumbered
  [85]–[95]).
```

> AGENT

Thoughts:

- **Yes** to `/notes/chores/` (not top-level `/chores/`) — keeps todo / done / README / chores in one umbrella; the filename `chores-NN.md` already telegraphs membership.
- **Preserve filenames** as `chores-NN.md` rather than renaming to `NN.md`. Section-anchor slugs (`#chore-open-...`) are unaffected by directory moves; preserving names means 246 refs are a clean `/notes/chores-NN.md` → `/notes/chores/chores-NN.md` substitution. Renaming to `NN.md` doubles the rename effort with no real win.
- **Wrinkle**: within each moved chores file, refs to *non-chores* siblings (`todo.md`, `done.md`, `README.md`, `../ARCHITECTURE.md`) need a `../` prefix. Refs to *other chores files* stay relative-as-is (still siblings, just in the new dir).
- **Scope**: 246 references across 24 files — mostly mechanical, but src/*.rs has GitHub URL fragments that need the same substitution.
- **Commit shape**: this doesn't belong inside `0.51.0-0` (cycle opener, plan-only design). It's a separate mechanical refactor — own commit, own version bump. Cleanest is to land 0.51.0-0 first, then do the doc move as `0.51.0-0.1` (sub-step) or — cleaner — as a 0.51.0-1 sibling before the sb_ide work starts (since the cycle's chores section is in chores-11.md which moves too).

Recommendation: push `0.51.0-0` as-proposed (no doc move yet); land the directory move as its own commit immediately after. Want me to push 0.51.0-0 now, then start on the move?

Yes — and the pivot from current uncommitted state is mostly mechanical:

- **Cargo.toml**: `0.51.0-0` → `0.51.0` (single-step now)
- **chores-11.md**: rewrite content from the sb_ide opener to a docs-reshape entry; the file itself moves to `notes/chores/chores-11.md` in this commit
- **todo.md**: drop the sb_ide `## In Progress` ladder (defer to 0.52.0 cycle); the done.md migration ride-along stays
- **The move itself**: `git mv notes/chores-NN.md notes/chores/chores-NN.md` for 1..11; mechanical ref substitution across the 24 files I identified earlier
- **sb_ide plan discarded**: the design I drafted is short enough to re-author when 0.52.0 opens; less awkward than parking it in chores-11

One question on shape: **single-step `0.51.0`** (one commit lands the whole move + ref substitution), or **multi-step `0.51.0-N`** (e.g. -0 = create dir + move files; -1 = update refs; -2 = close)?

I'd recommend **single-step** — the move and the refs have to land together anyway (intermediate state breaks anchor links). Multi-step would just create an inconsistent intermediate commit. OK with single-step?

  "content": "# Chores-11\n\nContinuation of `chores-10.md` (which is closed at `0.50.0` —\nthe Subcommand trait sweep). This file covers the `0.51.0`\ncycle onward. Reference numbering is file-local — see\n[`README.md`](../README.md#reference-numbering); chores-11\nstarts at `[1]`.\n\n## chore: relocate chores under notes/chores/ (0.51.0)\n\nSingle-step. `notes/` was dominated by ten chores files plus\nthe freshly opened `chores-11.md`; the eleven files now move\nunder `notes/chores/`, leaving `notes/` for the live notes\n(`todo.md`, `done.md`, `README.md`, design docs). Filenames\nstay `chores-NN.md` — preserving them keeps section-anchor\nslugs unchanged and reduces the cross-file ref update to a\nsingle mechanical substitution.\n\nDone.md hygiene rides along: the eleven `0.44.0`–`0.50.0`\nentries that had been sitting in `todo.md > ## Done` migrate\ninto `done.md` (renumbered `[85]`–`[95]`), since the 0.50.0\nclose-out marks a natural batching point and keeps `todo.md`\nfocused on what's actually in flight.\n\n### Substitutions applied\n\nExternal callers (everything outside `notes/chores/`):\n\n- `/notes/chores-NN.md` → `/notes/chores/chores-NN.md` —\n  absolute-path refs in `notes/todo.md`, `notes/done.md`,\n  `notes/README.md`, the sibling design notes, `CLAUDE.md`,\n  `ARCHITECTURE.md`, the root `README.md`, and the\n  GitHub-URL fragments in `src/clone.rs` /\n  `src/config.rs` / `src/init.rs` / `src/init/params.rs`\n  / `src/push.rs` / `src/scope.rs` / `src/subcommand.rs` /\n  `src/sync.rs`.\n\nWithin-chores siblings (now under `notes/chores/`):\n\n- `(../ARCHITECTURE.md` → `(../../ARCHITECTURE.md` (the\n  root `ARCHITECTURE.md` is two `..` away now).\n- `(../src/…` → `(../../src/…`.\n- `(README.md` → `(../README.md` (sibling\n  `notes/README.md`).\n- `(forks-multi-user.md` → `(../forks-multi-user.md` and\n  the `[1]: forks-multi-user.md` ref def in\n  `chores-08.md` (similar fixups for\n  `vc-x1-init.md` / `cargo-locked-issue.md`).\n\nIn-chores cross-refs to other chores files (now siblings\nin the same `chores/` dir) — `[chores-NN.md](chores-NN.md#…)`\nstyle — are unchanged.\n\n### Why this shape\n\n`/notes/chores/` (under notes/) rather than top-level\n`/chores/` keeps the notes umbrella intact —\n`todo.md` / `done.md` / `README.md` are conceptually\nadjacent to chores and benefit from sharing the\ndirectory. The `chores-` filename prefix is technically\nredundant once the files live in `chores/` (could become\n`01.md` / `02.md` / …), but the rename was rejected to\nkeep the diff focused: section-anchor slugs are\nfilename-independent, so the rename would buy no\nref-resolution simplification and would force renumbering\nof every `[N]:` URL slug in `todo.md`, `done.md`, and the\nsrc `.rs` GitHub fragments.\n\n# References\n"
}

20	Detailed motivation, safety requirements, and ordering belong
21	in `notes/chores/chores-NN.md` design subsections; link via `[N]` ref.
22	
23	Items use lazy numbering — every entry begins with `1. `; the
24	markdown renderer auto-numbers them, so reorder/insert without
25	renumbering. Reference by displayed number ("let's work on #3").
26	1. **single-field `options_flags` leaves → `value` field.**
27	   `0.47.0` introduced the convention (single-field leaf names
28	   its field `value`, declares the flag via `#[arg(long = "…")]`,
29	   so consumers read `args.<leaf>.value` not `args.<leaf>.<leaf>`)
30	   on the new `squash` leaf. Sweep the pre-existing single-field
31	   leaves to match: `scope`, `repo`, `dry_run`, `private`,
32	   `account`, `config`, `use_template` + their consumers
33	   (`init.rs`, tests).
34	1. **Investigate `linkme` for subcommand registration.**
35	   Distributed-slice registry — each subcommand registers itself
36	   at link time; `main.rs` discovers them via the slice rather
37	   than matching a `Commands` enum. Reduces per-subcommand
38	   touchpoints from 3 (mod decl + enum variant + match arm) to 1
39	   (registration). Costs: loses compile-time exhaustiveness
40	   (missing registration = runtime gap); help-output ordering
41	   depends on link order unless sorted; macro-magic dependency.
42	   Revisit once the `0.50.0` trait sweep's per-arm cost has been
43	   felt under real "add a subcommand" load.
44	   <https://github.com/dtolnay/linkme>
```

```
Single-step docs reshape. `notes/` was dominated by ten
chores files plus the freshly opened `chores-11.md`;
the eleven files now move under `notes/chores/`,
leaving `notes/` for live notes (`todo.md`, `done.md`,
`README.md`, design docs). Filenames stay `chores-NN.md`
— preserving them keeps section-anchor slugs unchanged
and reduces the cross-file ref update to a single
mechanical substitution.

Done.md hygiene rides along: the eleven 0.44.0 → 0.50.0
entries that had been sitting in `todo.md > ## Done`
migrate into `done.md` (renumbered [85]–[95]). See
`notes/chores/chores-11.md > chore: move chores under
notes/chores/ (0.51.0)` for the full record (incl. the
within-chores `../` prefix fixups for sibling notes
refs).

- Cargo.toml: 0.50.0 → 0.51.0.
- notes/chores-*.md → notes/chores/chores-*.md (11
  files moved; jj tracks the renames).
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: new docs-reshape chores
  section (replaces the sb_ide opener stub).
- Cross-file ref updates: `notes/chores-NN.md` →
  `notes/chores/chores-NN.md` across todo.md, done.md,
  CLAUDE.md, ARCHITECTURE.md, README.md (root),
  notes/README.md, notes/init-clone-refactor-conflict.md,
  notes/por-dual-parity.md, and the GitHub-URL
  fragments in src/{clone,config,init,init/params,push,
  scope,subcommand,sync}.rs.
- Within-chores: `../ARCHITECTURE.md` →
  `../../ARCHITECTURE.md`; `../src/…` → `../../src/…`;
  sibling notes refs (`README.md`, `forks-multi-user.md`,
  `vc-x1-init.md`, `cargo-locked-issue.md`) prefixed
  with `../`.
- notes/done.md: 11 entries appended (renumbered
  [85]–[95]) — the 0.44.0–0.50.0 batch migrated.
- notes/todo.md: ## Done batch deleted (migration
  noted); old refs [1], [2], [3], [30]–[37] removed;
  new entry for the 0.51.0 reshape with [1] ref; sb_ide
  elimination added as a ## Todo item for a future
  cycle.

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "keep the diff focused: section-anchor slugs are\nfilename-independent, so the rename would buy no\nref-resolution simplification and would force renumbering\nof every `[N]:` URL slug in `todo.md`, `done.md`, and the\nsrc `.rs` GitHub fragments.\n\n# References",
  "new_string": "keep the diff focused: section-anchor slugs are\nfilename-independent, so the rename would buy no\nref-resolution simplification and would force renumbering\nof every `[N]:` URL slug in `todo.md`, `done.md`, and the\nsrc `.rs` GitHub fragments.\n\n## chore: open sb_ide elimination (0.52.0-0)\n\nMulti-step. The `0.50.0` cycle landed every subcommand on\n`SubcommandRunner::dispatch` with two trait peek methods\n(`suppress_banner` / `is_detached_exec`) and one free\nfunction (`sb_ide`) carrying per-run \"session chrome\" —\nthe `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner, `finalize::surface_previous_failures`,\nand the `bm_track` enter/exit pair. This cycle eliminates\nall three by relocating each behavior to where it actually\nbelongs:\n\n- The `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner already lands on `--help` via\n  clap's `before_help`; printing it again at the top of\n  every normal run is duplicate chatter the user can\n  recover from `vc-x1 -V`. Drop the on-every-run banner.\n- `finalize::surface_previous_failures` is a finalize-side\n  concern; moving its trigger into `finalize` itself\n  (rather than into a generic dispatch hook) cleans up the\n  gate.\n- `bm_track` enter/exit is a diagnostic; demoting it to\n  `debug!` matches the \"permanent sanity check, but quiet\n  by default\" intent already recorded in its module doc.\n\nAfter these relocations the trait's `suppress_banner` /\n`is_detached_exec` methods have no remaining consumers,\nand `sb_ide` has no body left to keep — all three\ndisappear. Plan-only opener; the work lands in the\nsubsteps below.\n\n### What goes away — and the reasoning\n\n- **`bm_track` info-level emission.** Currently emits\n  `bm-track enter vc-x1 X: app(main)=tracked, …` on every\n  run. The module doc already notes \"a future refinement\n  would remove the steady-state noise without losing\n  detection value\" — `debug!` accomplishes exactly that.\n  Detection value is preserved (run with `-v` when\n  investigating); steady-state output gets cleaner.\n- **`sb_ide`'s banner-on-every-run.** `clap::Command::before_help`\n  already shows the banner on `--help`. The on-every-run\n  emission is duplicate; users who want it explicitly have\n  `vc-x1 -V`. Removing it shrinks the default output by one\n  line per command.\n- **`finalize::surface_previous_failures` gated in `sb_ide`.**\n  This is finalize machinery — it reads\n  `.vc-x1/finalize-fail-*` markers and surfaces them. The\n  \"don't surface from the detached child\" gate is\n  finalize's concern, not the trait's; moving the trigger\n  into `finalize` itself lets the gate live where the\n  marker logic lives.\n- **`SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec`.** With `bm_track`\n  at `debug!` and `surface_previous_failures` relocated,\n  no remaining consumer needs the trait's detached-exec\n  peek. Drop the method; `finalize` reads `params.exec`\n  directly as a finalize-private concern.\n- **`SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner`.** With the banner\n  gone from `sb_ide`, the `-L` flag still suppresses\n  bookmark labels in the listing subcommands' output\n  (chid/desc/list/show via `CommonParams::header`) — that\n  already lives in the op layer, not the chrome layer.\n  The trait peek has no remaining consumer; drop it,\n  along with the `suppress_banner` field on `ChidParams`\n  / `DescParams` / `ListParams` / `ShowParams`.\n\n### Ladder\n\n- 0.52.0-0 plan + version bump + this section + todo\n  ladder (current)\n- 0.52.0-1 `bm_track` → `debug!`; drop the bm_track gates\n  in `SubcommandRunner::dispatch`\n- 0.52.0-2..N eliminate `sb_ide`:\n  - Drop banner-on-every-run from `sb_ide`\n  - Move `finalize::surface_previous_failures` trigger\n    into `finalize` itself\n  - Delete `sb_ide` once both bodies have moved\n- 0.52.0-K drop `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec` and\n  `SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner` (no remaining\n  consumers); drop `suppress_banner` fields from the\n  four `Params` types\n- 0.52.0 close-out\n\n### Per-step evaluation\n\nEffectiveness is evaluated after each substep. Possible\noutcomes at any step boundary:\n\n- Continue as planned.\n- Significantly modify the shape (e.g. keep the banner\n  somewhere else; preserve `is_detached_exec` for a use\n  case discovered mid-cycle) — recorded in a new chores\n  subsection at the next step.\n- Abandon: revert the cycle to a non-eliminated\n  baseline, closing as a no-op with an `### Outcome` note\n  capturing what didn't fit.\n\n# References\n\n[1]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/commit/1e7c979e5458 \"1e7c979e5458189e4a5f380b18acd81d75ffe68b\""
}

Proposed commit title and body:

```
chore: open sb_ide elimination (0.52.0-0)

```
Multi-step cycle opener. The 0.50.0 cycle landed every
subcommand on `SubcommandRunner::dispatch` with two
trait peek methods (`suppress_banner` /
`is_detached_exec`) and one free function (`sb_ide`)
carrying per-run "session chrome" — the banner,
`finalize::surface_previous_failures`, and `bm_track`
enter/exit. This cycle eliminates all three by
relocating each behavior where it actually belongs:
banner already lives on `--help` (drop the on-every-run
emission), `surface_previous_failures` moves into
finalize, `bm_track` → `debug!`. After the relocations
the trait peeks have no remaining consumers and
`sb_ide` has no body left to keep.

Plan-only commit; the work lands across 0.52.0-N
substeps. See `notes/chores/chores-11.md > chore: open
sb_ide elimination (0.52.0-0)` for the full design +
ladder.

- Cargo.toml: 0.51.0 → 0.52.0-0.
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: backfilled `Commits: [[1]]`
  on the 0.51.0 docs-reshape section; new opener
  section for the sb_ide elimination cycle.
- notes/todo.md: new In Progress block + ladder; the
  earlier ## Todo entry covering this work removed
  (now In Progress).
```

252	pub fn bm_track(phase: &str, command_name: &str) {
253	    let header = format!("bm-track {phase} vc-x1 {command_name}");
254	    let root = match common::find_workspace_root() {
255	        Some(r) => r,
256	        None => {
257	            log::info!("{header}: no-workspace");
258	            return;
259	        }
260	    };
261	    let repos: [(std::path::PathBuf, &str); 2] =
262	        [(root.clone(), "app"), (root.join(".claude"), ".claude")];
263	    let mut parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
264	    for (repo, label) in repos {
265	        if !repo.join(".jj").exists() {
266	            parts.push(format!("{label}(main)=no-jj"));
267	            continue;
268	        }
269	        match bm_track_one(&repo, "main", "origin") {
270	            Ok(true) => parts.push(format!("{label}(main)=tracked")),
271	            Ok(false) => parts.push(format!("{label}(main)=NOT_TRACKED")),
272	            Err(e) => parts.push(format!(
273	                "{label}(main)=err({})",
274	                e.lines().next().unwrap_or("")
275	            )),
276	        }
277	    }
278	    log::info!("{header}: {}", parts.join(", "));
279	}

281	/// Query jj for whether `bookmark` in `repo` is tracking `remote`.
282	/// Returns `Ok(true)` if the tracked-list entry for `bookmark` shows
283	/// an `@<remote>` line (in any form — `@origin:` when synced, or
284	/// `@origin (ahead by N commits):` / similar when divergent — both
285	/// still count as tracking). `Ok(false)` when no such line exists
286	/// (bookmark isn't tracking this remote or doesn't exist). `Err` on
287	/// subprocess failure.
288	fn bm_track_one(repo: &Path, bookmark: &str, remote: &str) -> Result<bool, String> {
289	    let repo_str = repo.to_string_lossy();
290	    let output = std::process::Command::new("jj")
291	        .args(["bookmark", "list", "--tracked", bookmark, "-R", &repo_str])

235	/// Permanent sanity check for the `main`-bookmark tracking state
236	/// in both repos of the dual-repo workspace. Prints one line on
237	/// entry and one on exit of every command. If entry and exit
238	/// differ, the executing command is the culprit; if entry differs
239	/// from the previous command's exit, something *between*
240	/// invocations broke it. Originally added in 0.37.2 as a temporary
241	/// diagnostic; promoted to permanent in 0.37.4 after the user
242	/// reported "happens more than once" — the signal is worth keeping
243	/// in place until confidence in its correctness is well-established
244	/// across varied use, at which point a "silent when clean"
245	/// refinement would remove the steady-state noise without losing
246	/// detection value (see `notes/todo.md`).
247	///
248	/// Walks up from cwd to locate the workspace root (the directory
249	/// whose `.vc-config.toml` has `path = "/"`), then probes `<root>`
250	/// and `<root>/.claude`. Same labeling whether the user runs from
251	/// the app root, from `.claude`, or from any subdir.

Originally added in 0.37.2 as a temporary\n/// diagnostic; promoted to permanent in 0.37.4 after the user\n/// reported \"happens more than once\" — the signal is worth keeping\n/// in place until confidence in its correctness is well-established\n/// across varied use, at which point a \"silent when clean\"\n/// refinement would remove the steady-state noise without losing\n/// detection value (see `notes/todo.md`).\n///\n/// Walks up from cwd to locate the workspace root (the directory\n/// whose `.vc-config.toml` has `path = \"/\"`), then probes `<root>`\n/// and `<root>/.claude`. Same labeling whether the user runs from\n/// the app root, from `.claude`, or from any subdir.\npub fn bm_track(phase: &str, command_name: &str) {\n    let header = format!(\"bm-track {phase} vc-x1 {command_name}\");\n    let root = match common::find_workspace_root() {\n        Some(r) => r,\n        None => {\n            log::info!(\"{header}: no-workspace\");\n            return;\n        }\n    };\n    let repos: [(std::path::PathBuf, &str); 2] =\n        [(root.clone(), \"app\"), (root.join(\".claude\"), \".claude\")];\n    let mut parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    for (repo, label) in repos {\n        if !repo.join(\".jj\").exists() {\n            parts.push(format!(\"{label}(main)=no-jj\"));\n            continue;\n        }\n        match bm_track_one(&repo, \"main\", \"origin\") {\n            Ok(true) => parts.push(format!(\"{label}(main)=tracked\")),\n            Ok(false) => parts.push(format!(\"{label}(main)=NOT_TRACKED\")),\n            Err(e) => parts.push(format!(\n                \"{label}(main)=err({})\",\n                e.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\")\n            )),\n        }\n    }\n    log::info!(\"{header}: {}\", parts.join(\", \"));\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Permanent sanity check for the `main`-bookmark tracking state\n/// in both repos of the dual-repo workspace. Emits one line on\n/// entry and one on exit of every command. If entry and exit\n/// differ, the executing command is the culprit; if entry differs\n/// from the previous command's exit, something *between*\n/// invocations broke it. Originally added in 0.37.2 as a temporary\n/// diagnostic; promoted to permanent in 0.37.4 after the user\n/// reported \"happens more than once\".\n///\n/// Emits at `log::debug!` (since 0.52.0-1) — default runs stay\n/// quiet, and the signal remains available under `-v` when\n/// investigating. The detached `finalize --exec` child runs at\n/// default verbosity so it stays silent without needing a special\n/// gate at the call site.\n///\n/// Walks up from cwd to locate the workspace root (the directory\n/// whose `.vc-config.toml` has `path = \"/\"`), then probes `<root>`\n/// and `<root>/.claude`. Same labeling whether the user runs from\n/// the app root, from `.claude`, or from any subdir.\npub fn bm_track(phase: &str, command_name: &str) {\n    let header = format!(\"bm-track {phase} vc-x1 {command_name}\");\n    let root = match common::find_workspace_root() {\n        Some(r) => r,\n        None => {\n            log::debug!(\"{header}: no-workspace\");\n            return;\n        }\n    };\n    let repos: [(std::path::PathBuf, &str); 2] =\n        [(root.clone(), \"app\"), (root.join(\".claude\"), \".claude\")];\n    let mut parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    for (repo, label) in repos {\n        if !repo.join(\".jj\").exists() {\n            parts.push(format!(\"{label}(main)=no-jj\"));\n            continue;\n        }\n        match bm_track_one(&repo, \"main\", \"origin\") {\n            Ok(true) => parts.push(format!(\"{label}(main)=tracked\")),\n            Ok(false) => parts.push(format!(\"{label}(main)=NOT_TRACKED\")),\n            Err(e) => parts.push(format!(\n                \"{label}(main)=err({})\",\n                e.lines().next().unwrap_or(\"\")\n            )),\n        }\n    }\n    log::debug!(\"{header}: {}\", parts.join(\", \"));\n}"
}

75	    fn is_detached_exec(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
76	        false
77	    }
78	
79	    /// Default dispatch: build `Params` via `to_params`, emit
80	    /// session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`], bracket the run with
81	    /// `crate::bm_track` enter/exit (skipped when
82	    /// `is_detached_exec`), execute via `run`, and map the result
83	    /// to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via `error!` and
84	    /// return `ExitCode::FAILURE`.
85	    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {
86	        let params = match self.to_params() {
87	            Ok(p) => p,
88	            Err(e) => {
89	                error!("{e}");
90	                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
91	            }
92	        };
93	        let is_detached = Self::is_detached_exec(&params);
94	        crate::sb_ide(Self::suppress_banner(&params), is_detached);
95	
96	        // Command name is the first positional after the binary;
97	        // clap has already validated it by the time we reach
98	        // dispatch (top-level parse errors exit earlier).
99	        let command_name = std::env::args().nth(1).unwrap_or_else(|| "?".to_string()); // OK: default when somehow invoked without a subcommand
100	
101	        if !is_detached {
102	            crate::bm_track("enter", &command_name);
103	        }
104	        let exit_code = match Self::run(ctx, &params) {
105	            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
106	            Err(e) => {
107	                error!("{e}");
108	                ExitCode::FAILURE
109	            }
110	        };
111	        if !is_detached {
112	            crate::bm_track("exit ", &command_name);
113	        }
114	        exit_code
115	    }
116	}
117

  "old_string": "    /// Default dispatch: build `Params` via `to_params`, emit\n    /// session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`], bracket the run with\n    /// `crate::bm_track` enter/exit (skipped when\n    /// `is_detached_exec`), execute via `run`, and map the result\n    /// to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via `error!` and\n    /// return `ExitCode::FAILURE`.\n    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {\n        let params = match self.to_params() {\n            Ok(p) => p,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                return ExitCode::FAILURE;\n            }\n        };\n        let is_detached = Self::is_detached_exec(&params);\n        crate::sb_ide(Self::suppress_banner(&params), is_detached);\n\n        // Command name is the first positional after the binary;\n        // clap has already validated it by the time we reach\n        // dispatch (top-level parse errors exit earlier).\n        let command_name = std::env::args().nth(1).unwrap_or_else(|| \"?\".to_string()); // OK: default when somehow invoked without a subcommand\n\n        if !is_detached {\n            crate::bm_track(\"enter\", &command_name);\n        }\n        let exit_code = match Self::run(ctx, &params) {\n            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                ExitCode::FAILURE\n            }\n        };\n        if !is_detached {\n            crate::bm_track(\"exit \", &command_name);\n        }\n        exit_code\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Default dispatch: build `Params` via `to_params`, emit\n    /// session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`], bracket the run with\n    /// `crate::bm_track` enter/exit, execute via `run`, and map\n    /// the result to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via\n    /// `error!` and return `ExitCode::FAILURE`. `bm_track` itself\n    /// emits at `debug!`, so default runs stay quiet and the\n    /// detached `finalize --exec` child (which runs without `-v`)\n    /// silently no-ops — no per-call gate is needed here.\n    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {\n        let params = match self.to_params() {\n            Ok(p) => p,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                return ExitCode::FAILURE;\n            }\n        };\n        crate::sb_ide(\n            Self::suppress_banner(&params),\n            Self::is_detached_exec(&params),\n        );\n\n        // Command name is the first positional after the binary;\n        // clap has already validated it by the time we reach\n        // dispatch (top-level parse errors exit earlier).\n        let command_name = std::env::args().nth(1).unwrap_or_else(|| \"?\".to_string()); // OK: default when somehow invoked without a subcommand\n\n        crate::bm_track(\"enter\", &command_name);\n        let exit_code = match Self::run(ctx, &params) {\n            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,\n            Err(e) => {\n                error!(\"{e}\");\n                ExitCode::FAILURE\n            }\n        };\n        crate::bm_track(\"exit \", &command_name);\n        exit_code\n    }"
}

> AGENT

Proposed commit title and body:

```
refactor: bm_track → debug! (0.52.0-1)
```

```
First sb_ide-elimination substep. `bm_track` enter/exit
lines were emitted at `log::info!`, lighting up on every
command run; demote them to `log::debug!` so default
runs stay quiet and the signal remains available under
`-v` when investigating.

With the demotion the `if !is_detached { bm_track(…) }`
gates in `SubcommandRunner::dispatch` are unnecessary —
the detached `finalize --exec` child runs at default
verbosity, so debug-level output silently no-ops in
that path too. The gates collapse; `is_detached_exec`
survives the substep purely for `sb_ide`'s remaining
banner / `surface_previous_failures` gate, which the
next substeps will dismantle. See
`notes/chores/chores-11.md > refactor: bm_track →
debug! (0.52.0-1)` for the full record.

- Cargo.toml: 0.52.0-0 → 0.52.0-1.
- src/main.rs: bm_track's two log::info! calls →
  log::debug!; doc-comment reworded.
- src/subcommand.rs: dispatch drops the two
  `if !is_detached { bm_track(…) }` gates.
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: backfilled
  `Commits: [[2]]` on the 0.52.0-0 opener; new
  0.52.0-1 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.52.0-0 → (done).
```

OK to push?

> DEVELOPER

One other change maybe now or a little later. Rather than outputing the version everytime we output it only with -V but support -V on all commands

> AGENT

Good companion change. Recommendation: **defer to 0.52.0-2** (the banner-removal substep), not squash into 0.52.0-1.

Reasoning:

- 0.52.0-1 is the `bm_track` demotion — single-purpose, already coherent. Adding `-V` propagation would dilute the substep.
- The natural pairing is **banner removal + `-V` on subcommands** as 0.52.0-2: users lose one way of seeing the version (the on-every-run banner) and gain another (`-V` on any subcommand). Both bullets in one commit make the trade-off obvious in the diff.
- Mechanically clap supports this via `propagate_version = true` on the top-level `#[command(...)]` attribute — single-flag change, then per-subcommand `-V` works.

Push 0.52.0-1 as-proposed, then I open 0.52.0-2 with both changes (drop sb_ide banner + propagate_version)?

> DEVELOPER

push 0.52.0-1, then start 0.52.0-2

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/main.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Session chrome: emit the leading banner (unless suppressed) and\n/// surface any failures left over from the previous run, gated on\n/// \"this isn't the detached `finalize --exec` re-entry\".\n///\n/// Called from the trait's default `dispatch` (for ported\n/// subcommands, reading the bools from each command's `Params`)\n/// and from each unported arm in `fn main()` (reading the bools\n/// from the per-arm peek match). When all arms are ported, the\n/// `main`-side callers disappear; only the trait callers remain.\npub fn sb_ide(suppress_banner: bool, is_detached_exec: bool) {\n    if !is_detached_exec {\n        if !suppress_banner {\n            // Banner on every normal run, mirroring what `--help`\n            // shows at the top.\n            log::info!(\"{BANNER}\");\n        }\n        finalize::surface_previous_failures();\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Surface failures left over from the previous run, gated on\n/// \"this isn't the detached `finalize --exec` re-entry\" (the\n/// detached child's log isn't where users see surfaced failures).\n///\n/// Called from the trait's default `dispatch`. Banner emission\n/// used to live here too but was dropped in 0.52.0-2 — clap's\n/// `before_help` already shows `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` on `--help`, and\n/// `propagate_version = true` makes `-V` work on every\n/// subcommand, so the on-every-run emission was duplicate\n/// chatter. The `surface_previous_failures` body is finalize\n/// machinery; folding it into finalize itself is the next\n/// substep, after which `sb_ide` and `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec`\n/// disappear.\npub fn sb_ide(is_detached_exec: bool) {\n    if !is_detached_exec {\n        finalize::surface_previous_failures();\n    }\n}"
}
```

  "old_string": "/// Clap-free params for `chid`; embeds resolved `CommonParams`.\n///\n/// Carries a `suppress_banner` flag (read off `-L` / `--no-label`\n/// at the binary edge) so the trait's default `dispatch` can\n/// query it through `SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner` without\n/// re-touching clap.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct ChidParams {\n    pub common: CommonParams,\n    pub suppress_banner: bool,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&ChidArgs> for ChidParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Resolve clap `ChidArgs` into `ChidParams` by delegating to\n    /// `CommonParams::try_from` and copying the no-label flag\n    /// into `suppress_banner`.\n    fn try_from(a: &ChidArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        Ok(ChidParams {\n            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,\n            suppress_banner: a.common.no_label,\n        })\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Clap-free params for `chid`; embeds resolved `CommonParams`.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct ChidParams {\n    pub common: CommonParams,\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<&ChidArgs> for ChidParams {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    /// Resolve clap `ChidArgs` into `ChidParams` by delegating to\n    /// `CommonParams::try_from`; `chid` has no fields beyond\n    /// `CommonArgs`.\n    fn try_from(a: &ChidArgs) -> Result<Self, String> {\n        Ok(ChidParams {\n            common: CommonParams::try_from(&a.common)?,\n        })\n    }\n}"
}

> AGENT

Proposed commit title and body:

```
refactor: drop banner-on-every-run (0.52.0-2)
```

```
Second sb_ide-elimination substep. `sb_ide` no longer
emits `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` on every command run — clap already
shows it via `before_help` on `--help`, and
`propagate_version = true` (already in place from the
0.50.0 cycle) makes `vc-x1 <subcommand> -V` work too.
The on-every-run emission was duplicate chatter.

With the banner gone, `sb_ide`'s `suppress_banner`
parameter loses its only consumer; the
`SubcommandRunner::suppress_banner` trait method and
the `suppress_banner` field on the four listing
`Params` types (`ChidParams`, `DescParams`,
`ListParams`, `ShowParams`) cascade out. `-L` /
`--no-label` still works — it suppresses per-repo
header labels in op-layer output, a concern that was
always separate from banner suppression. See
`notes/chores/chores-11.md > refactor: drop
banner-on-every-run (0.52.0-2)` for the full record.

`sb_ide` retains one remaining responsibility: gating
`finalize::surface_previous_failures`. The next
substep moves that into finalize itself, after which
`sb_ide` and `is_detached_exec` both disappear.

- Cargo.toml: 0.52.0-1 → 0.52.0-2.
- src/main.rs: sb_ide body drops banner emission;
  signature drops `suppress_banner` param.
- src/subcommand.rs: dispatch call updated; trait
  method `suppress_banner` removed.
- src/chid.rs / src/desc.rs / src/list.rs /
  src/show.rs: `suppress_banner` field on each
  `Params` removed; `TryFrom` impls drop the
  assignment; trait override removed.
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: backfilled
  `Commits: [[3]]` on the 0.52.0-1 section; new
  0.52.0-2 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.52.0-1 → (done); ladder reworked
  (-K reduced to is_detached_exec only).
```

OK to push?

295	/// subprocess failure.
296	fn bm_track_one(repo: &Path, bookmark: &str, remote: &str) -> Result<bool, String> {
297	    let repo_str = repo.to_string_lossy();
298	    let output = std::process::Command::new("jj")
299	        .args(["bookmark", "list", "--tracked", bookmark, "-R", &repo_str])
300	        .output()
301	        .map_err(|e| format!("spawn: {e}"))?;
302	    if !output.status.success() {
303	        return Err(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).trim().to_string());
304	    }
305	    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout);
306	    // Accept both the synced form (`@origin:`) and the decorated
307	    // divergent form (`@origin (ahead by N commits):`), which still
308	    // represents a tracking relationship.
309	    let colon = format!("@{remote}:");
310	    let paren = format!("@{remote} ");
311	    Ok(stdout.lines().any(|l| {
312	        let t = l.trim_start();
313	        t.starts_with(&colon) || t.starts_with(&paren)
314	    }))
315	}
316	
317	fn main() -> ExitCode {
318	    CompleteEnv::with_factory(cli_with_banner).complete();
319	    let matches = cli_with_banner().get_matches();
320	    let cli = match Cli::from_arg_matches(&matches) {
321	        Ok(cli) => cli,
322	        Err(e) => {
323	            e.exit();
324	        }
325	    };
326	
327	    let log_path = cli.log.as_ref().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());
328	    logging::CliLogger::init(cli.verbose, log_path.as_deref());
329	
330	    let ctx = match context::Context::load(cli.log) {
331	        Ok(c) => c,
332	        Err(e) => {
333	            error!("{e}");
334	            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
335	        }
336	    };
337	
338	    match cli.command {
339	        Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
340	        Commands::Desc(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
341	        Commands::List(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),

  "new_string": "    let log_path = cli.log.as_ref().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());\n    logging::CliLogger::init(cli.verbose, log_path.as_deref());\n\n    // `-V` / `--version`: emit the banner as the first line and\n    // continue. With no subcommand, the banner is the whole\n    // invocation. The subcommand-suffixed form\n    // (`vc-x1-<sub> X.Y.Z`) mirrors what clap's now-disabled\n    // auto-version + `propagate_version` used to print.\n    if cli.version {\n        match matches.subcommand_name() {\n            Some(name) => {\n                log::info!(\"vc-x1-{name} {}\", env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\"));\n            }\n            None => log::info!(\"{BANNER}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    let Some(cmd) = cli.command else {\n        // No subcommand. If `-V` was set the banner has already\n        // printed, so exit success; otherwise mirror clap's \"a\n        // subcommand is required\" error by printing usage and\n        // exiting non-zero.\n        if cli.version {\n            return ExitCode::SUCCESS;\n        }\n        let mut cmd = cli_with_banner();\n        let _ = cmd.print_help();\n        return ExitCode::FAILURE;\n    };\n\n    let ctx = match context::Context::load(cli.log) {\n        Ok(c) => c,\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"{e}\");\n            return ExitCode::FAILURE;\n        }\n    };\n\n    match cmd {\n        Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::Desc(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::List(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::Show(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::ValidateDesc(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::FixDesc(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::Clone(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::Init(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::Symlink(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::Sync(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::Finalize(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n        Commands::Push(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),\n    }\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Proposed commit title and body:

```
feat: -V toggles version banner (0.52.0-2)
```

```
sb_ide-elimination substep that replaces the
banner-on-every-run + clap-auto-version pairing with a
single `-V` banner-toggle:

- `vc-x1 chid -s code,bot` — runs silently.
- `vc-x1 chid -s code,bot -V` — prints
  `vc-x1-chid 0.52.0-2` as the first line, then runs.
- `vc-x1 -V` — prints `vc-x1 0.52.0-2`, exits success.

Scripts can now capture the version *and* the
command's output in one invocation; users who only
want the version still get a one-line answer.

With the banner gone from the on-every-run path,
sb_ide's `suppress_banner` parameter loses its only
consumer; the trait method and the field on the four
listing Params types cascade out. `-L` / `--no-label`
keeps working — it suppresses op-layer per-repo
headers (CommonParams::header), separate from banner
suppression all along. See
`notes/chores/chores-11.md > feat: -V toggles version
banner (0.52.0-2)` for the full record.

`sb_ide` retains one remaining responsibility — gating
`finalize::surface_previous_failures`. The next
substep moves that trigger into finalize itself, and
`sb_ide` + `is_detached_exec` disappear.

- Cargo.toml: 0.52.0-1 → 0.52.0-2.
- src/main.rs: Cli drops clap's auto-version,
  adds global `-V`/`--version` bool;
  `command` becomes Option<Commands>; main emits
  banner before dispatch + handles no-subcommand
  paths; sb_ide drops banner emission +
  `suppress_banner` param.
- src/subcommand.rs: dispatch call simplified;
  trait `suppress_banner` removed.
- src/chid.rs / src/desc.rs / src/list.rs /
  src/show.rs: `suppress_banner` field + override
  removed.
- src/clone.rs / src/finalize.rs / src/init/tests.rs:
  test helpers wrap `Commands::X` in `Some(...)`.
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: backfilled
  `Commits: [[3]]` on 0.52.0-1; new 0.52.0-2 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.52.0-1 → (done); ladder line
  reworded.
```

OK to push?

Updated commit body (banner mention adjusted):

```
feat: -V toggles version banner (0.52.0-2)
```

```
sb_ide-elimination substep that replaces the
banner-on-every-run + clap-auto-version pairing with a
single `-V` banner-toggle:

- `vc-x1 chid -s code,bot` — runs silently.
- `vc-x1 chid -s code,bot -V` — prints
  `vc-x1 0.52.0-2` as the first line, then runs.
- `vc-x1 -V` — prints `vc-x1 0.52.0-2`, exits success.

Scripts can now capture the version *and* the
command's output in one invocation; users who only
want the version still get a one-line answer. Banner
is uniform `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` regardless of subcommand —
the version is the binary's either way.

With the banner gone from the on-every-run path,
sb_ide's `suppress_banner` parameter loses its only
consumer; the trait method and the field on the four
listing Params types cascade out. `-L` / `--no-label`
keeps working — it suppresses op-layer per-repo
headers (CommonParams::header), separate from banner
suppression all along. See
`notes/chores/chores-11.md > feat: -V toggles version
banner (0.52.0-2)` for the full record.

`sb_ide` retains one remaining responsibility — gating
`finalize::surface_previous_failures`. The next
substep moves that trigger into finalize itself, and
`sb_ide` + `is_detached_exec` disappear.

- Cargo.toml: 0.52.0-1 → 0.52.0-2.
- src/main.rs: Cli drops clap's auto-version + adds
  global `-V`/`--version` bool; `command` becomes
  Option<Commands>; main emits banner before dispatch
  + handles no-subcommand paths; sb_ide drops banner
  emission + `suppress_banner` param.
- src/subcommand.rs: dispatch call simplified;
  trait `suppress_banner` removed.
- src/chid.rs / src/desc.rs / src/list.rs /
  src/show.rs: `suppress_banner` field + override
  removed.
- src/clone.rs / src/finalize.rs / src/init/tests.rs:
  test helpers wrap `Commands::X` in `Some(...)`.
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: backfilled
  `Commits: [[3]]` on 0.52.0-1; new 0.52.0-2 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.52.0-1 → (done); ladder reworded.
```

55	/// Read, print, and delete any failure markers left by previous detached
56	/// finalize children. Cheap no-op when the directory doesn't exist.
57	pub fn surface_previous_failures() {
58	    let Some(dir) = status_dir() else {
59	        return;
60	    };
61	    let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&dir) else {
62	        return;
63	    };
64	    let mut paths: Vec<PathBuf> = entries
65	        .flatten()
66	        .map(|e| e.path())
67	        .filter(|p| p.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some("status"))
68	        .collect();
69	    paths.sort();
70	    for path in paths {
71	        if let Ok(content) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
72	            eprintln!(
73	                "warn: previous finalize failure ({}):",
74	                path.file_stem()
75	                    .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy())
76	                    .unwrap_or_default() // OK: filename without extension; empty on unexpected absence
77	            );
78	            for line in content.lines() {
79	                eprintln!("  {line}");
80	            }
81	            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
82	        }
83	    }
84	}

> AGENT

`surface_previous_failures` is destructive (reads + deletes markers), so the gate must stay somewhere — but it doesn't have to be in `sb_ide`. Your proposal works cleanly with the gate inlined in `main`:

```rust
let is_exec = matches!(&cli.command, Some(Commands::Finalize(args)) if args.exec);
if !is_exec {
    finalize::surface_previous_failures();
}
```

That deletes:

- `sb_ide` entirely (its body becomes just this gate, moved inline)
- `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec` trait method
- finalize's `is_detached_exec` override (the impl on `FinalizeArgs`)

`main` gains a one-line `matches!` peek that reads `FinalizeArgs.exec` directly (the field is the "Param for finalize" you mentioned — it's already there from 0.50.0-6's finalize port). That peek is the only remaining knowledge of the detached-exec concept outside finalize itself.

After this, every goal the cycle set out for is met:

- ✅ Banner gone (or -V-triggered)
- ✅ bm_track debug-only
- ✅ sb_ide deleted
- ✅ Trait peek methods deleted
- ✅ suppress_banner cascade gone (already done in -2)

So this could land as **0.52.0-3 + close-out as one commit** (0.52.0, drop suffix) — the work is small enough and there's nothing else queued.

Want me to draft it as a single 0.52.0 close-out commit?

> DEVELOPER

What I want to do is remove is_detached_exec from `trait SubcommandRunner`

> AGENT

Right — that's the clean removal. The cascade is small but coupled:

1. Drop `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec` trait method.
2. Drop finalize's `is_detached_exec` override on `FinalizeArgs`.
3. Dispatch can no longer call `sb_ide(Self::is_detached_exec(...))` — and since `sb_ide`'s body is now just `if !is_detached_exec { surface_previous_failures() }`, the simplest move is to inline the gate in `main` and delete `sb_ide` entirely.

End state: `main` peeks `FinalizeArgs.exec` directly (the param is finalize's own field), calls `surface_previous_failures` before dispatch unless the exec child is running. The trait stays narrow (`to_params`, `run`, `dispatch`).

This effectively completes the cycle — after this, all the stated goals are met and only the close-out (drop `0.52.0-3` suffix, todo→done) remains.

Want me to land it as **0.52.0-3 + close-out folded into one commit** (single `0.52.0` no-suffix), or **0.52.0-3 first, then 0.52.0 close-out separately**?

> DEVELOPER

I vote just make this change -3 without closing out

```
1	//! `SubcommandRunner` trait
2	//!
3	//! The purpose is to simplify invoking a subcommand so `main`
4	//! can invoke any subcommand with one line:
5	//!
6	//! `Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),`
7	//!
8	//! Instead of the ~11 lines that the default `dispatch` below
9	//! now encapsulates.
10	//!
11	//! Implementors provide:
12	//!
13	//! - [`SubcommandRunner::to_params`] — build the clap-free
14	//!   `Params` from the clap `Args` (covers both `From` /
15	//!   `TryFrom` shapes uniformly via `Result<_, String>`).
16	//! - [`SubcommandRunner::run`] — the subcommand body.
17	//!
18	//! The default [`SubcommandRunner::dispatch`] builds `Params`
19	//! via `to_params`, runs via `run`, and maps the result to
20	//! `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via `error!` and return
21	//! `ExitCode::FAILURE`. `Context` is loaded once in `main` and
22	//! passed in by reference so a single `Context` is shared across
23	//! the (one) match arm that actually runs.
24	//!
25	//! ## See also
26	//!
27	//! - The wider CLI-args / subcommand-layer split this trait
28	//!   formalizes: [`ARCHITECTURE.md > args → Context + Params`][arch].
29	//! - Cycle design, ladder, evaluation gate:
30	//!   [`chores-10.md > 0.50.0-0`][open].
31	//! - Worked example, trait-shape decisions, naming:
32	//!   [`chores-10.md > 0.50.0-1`][port].
33	//! - First implementor: [`crate::chid`].
34	//!
35	//! [arch]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/ARCHITECTURE.md#args--context--params
36	//! [open]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/notes/chores/chores-10.md#chore-open-subcommand-trait-sweep-0500-0
37	//! [port]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/blob/main/notes/chores/chores-10.md#refactor-subcommandrunner-trait--chid-0500-1
38	
39	use std::process::ExitCode;
40	
41	use log::error;
42	
43	use crate::context::Context;
44	
45	/// Trait implemented by every subcommand's clap `Args` type so
46	/// `main.rs` can dispatch via a single `args.dispatch(&ctx)`
47	/// call.
48	pub trait SubcommandRunner {
49	    /// The clap-free `Params` struct the subcommand body
50	    /// consumes.
51	    type Params;
52	
53	    /// Build `Params` from `Args`. `Result<_, String>` absorbs
54	    /// both `From` (return `Ok(...)`) and `TryFrom` (forward
55	    /// the error) shapes uniformly.
56	    fn to_params(&self) -> Result<Self::Params, String>;
57	
58	    /// Run the subcommand body.
59	    fn run(ctx: &Context, params: &Self::Params) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>>;
60	
61	    /// Whether this invocation is the detached `finalize --exec`
62	    /// child. Default `false`; `finalize` overrides to read it
63	    /// from its `Params`. Consumed by `dispatch` for both the
64	    /// banner suppression (via `crate::sb_ide`) and the
65	    /// `bm_track` enter/exit gating (the detached child shouldn't
66	    /// emit user-facing bookmark-tracking lines).
67	    fn is_detached_exec(_params: &Self::Params) -> bool {
68	        false
69	    }
70	
71	    /// Default dispatch: build `Params` via `to_params`, emit
72	    /// session chrome via [`crate::sb_ide`], bracket the run with
73	    /// `crate::bm_track` enter/exit, execute via `run`, and map
74	    /// the result to `ExitCode`. Errors at any stage log via
75	    /// `error!` and return `ExitCode::FAILURE`. `bm_track` itself
76	    /// emits at `debug!`, so default runs stay quiet and the
77	    /// detached `finalize --exec` child (which runs without `-v`)
78	    /// silently no-ops — no per-call gate is needed here.
79	    fn dispatch(&self, ctx: &Context) -> ExitCode {
80	        let params = match self.to_params() {
81	            Ok(p) => p,
82	            Err(e) => {
83	                error!("{e}");
84	                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
85	            }
86	        };
87	        crate::sb_ide(Self::is_detached_exec(&params));
88	
89	        // Command name is the first positional after the binary;
90	        // clap has already validated it by the time we reach
91	        // dispatch (top-level parse errors exit earlier).
92	        let command_name = std::env::args().nth(1).unwrap_or_else(|| "?".to_string()); // OK: default when somehow invoked without a subcommand
93	
94	        crate::bm_track("enter", &command_name);
95	        let exit_code = match Self::run(ctx, &params) {
96	            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
97	            Err(e) => {
98	                error!("{e}");
99	                ExitCode::FAILURE
100	            }
101	        };
102	        crate::bm_track("exit ", &command_name);
103	        exit_code
104	    }
105	}
106

297	fn main() -> ExitCode {
298	    CompleteEnv::with_factory(cli_with_banner).complete();
299	    let matches = cli_with_banner().get_matches();
300	    let cli = match Cli::from_arg_matches(&matches) {
301	        Ok(cli) => cli,
302	        Err(e) => {
303	            e.exit();
304	        }
305	    };
306	
307	    let log_path = cli.log.as_ref().map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().to_string());
308	    logging::CliLogger::init(cli.verbose, log_path.as_deref());
309	
310	    // `-V` / `--version`: emit the `vc-x1 X.Y.Z` banner as the
311	    // first line and continue. With no subcommand, the banner is
312	    // the whole invocation. Uniform `BANNER` (not the
313	    // `vc-x1-<sub>` form clap's `propagate_version` would print)
314	    // — the version is the binary's regardless of which
315	    // subcommand it routes to.
316	    if cli.version {
317	        log::info!("{BANNER}");
318	    }
319	
320	    let Some(cmd) = cli.command else {
321	        // No subcommand. If `-V` was set the banner has already
322	        // printed, so exit success; otherwise mirror clap's "a
323	        // subcommand is required" error by printing usage and
324	        // exiting non-zero.
325	        if cli.version {
326	            return ExitCode::SUCCESS;
327	        }
328	        let mut cmd = cli_with_banner();
329	        let _ = cmd.print_help();
330	        return ExitCode::FAILURE;
331	    };
332	
333	    let ctx = match context::Context::load(cli.log) {
334	        Ok(c) => c,
335	        Err(e) => {
336	            error!("{e}");
337	            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
338	        }
339	    };
340	
341	    match cmd {
342	        Commands::Chid(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
343	        Commands::Desc(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
344	        Commands::List(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
345	        Commands::Show(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
346	        Commands::ValidateDesc(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
347	        Commands::FixDesc(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
348	        Commands::Clone(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
349	        Commands::Init(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
350	        Commands::Symlink(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
351	        Commands::Sync(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
352	        Commands::Finalize(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
353	        Commands::Push(args) => args.dispatch(&ctx),
354	    }

  "new_string": "## refactor: remove is_detached_exec from trait (0.52.0-3)\n\nThird substep. The `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec`\ntrait method goes away, taking `sb_ide` with it. The\ndetached-exec gate is finalize-specific machinery —\n`finalize::surface_previous_failures` is the only\nbehavior it ever protected, and `FinalizeArgs.exec` is\nthe only field that ever set it. Lifting the gate into\n`main` (one `matches!` against `Commands::Finalize`\nwith `args.exec`) cuts the trait peek + its sole\noverride + the free function that consumed it.\n\nAfter this substep:\n\n- The `SubcommandRunner` trait surface is the\n  load-bearing minimum: `to_params`, `run`, and the\n  default `dispatch`. No more peek methods.\n- `main` owns the surface-previous-failures call,\n  with the exec-child skip inline at the call site.\n  Single home for the gate; no indirection through\n  `sb_ide` or the trait.\n- `dispatch` shrinks to \"build params, bracket with\n  `bm_track`, run, map exit code.\" No session-chrome\n  responsibility at all.\n\nThe cycle's stated goal — eliminate `sb_ide` and both\ntrait peeks — is met after this substep. Close-out\nlands separately as `0.52.0` (todo→done, no code).\n\n- `Cargo.toml`: `0.52.0-2` → `0.52.0-3`.\n- `src/subcommand.rs`: `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec`\n  removed; `dispatch` drops its `crate::sb_ide(…)`\n  call; doc rewritten.\n- `src/finalize.rs`: `is_detached_exec` override on\n  `FinalizeArgs` removed.\n- `src/main.rs`: `pub fn sb_ide` deleted (no\n  callers); `main` adds an inline\n  `matches!(&cmd, Commands::Finalize(args) if args.exec)`\n  peek and calls `finalize::surface_previous_failures`\n  before loading `Context`.\n- `notes/chores/chores-11.md`: backfilled\n  `Commits: [[4]]` on the 0.52.0-2 section; new\n  0.52.0-3 section.\n- `notes/todo.md`: 0.52.0-2 → `(done)`; 0.52.0-3\n  marked `(current)`.\n\n# References\n\n[1]: https://github.com/winksaville/vc-x1/commit/1e7c979e5458 \"1e7c979e5458189e4a5f380b18acd81d75ffe68b\""
}

Proposed commit title and body:

```
refactor: remove is_detached_exec from trait (0.52.0-3)
```

```
Third sb_ide-elimination substep. The
`SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec` trait method goes
away, taking `sb_ide` with it. The detached-exec gate
is finalize-specific machinery —
`finalize::surface_previous_failures` is the only
behavior it ever protected, and `FinalizeArgs.exec`
is the only field that ever set it. Lifting the gate
into `main` (one `matches!` against
`Commands::Finalize` with `args.exec`) cuts the trait
peek + its sole override + the free function that
consumed it.

After this substep the `SubcommandRunner` trait
surface is the load-bearing minimum (`to_params`,
`run`, `dispatch`); `main` owns the
surface-previous-failures call with the exec-child
skip inline at the call site; `dispatch` has no
session-chrome responsibility at all. The cycle's
stated goal — eliminate `sb_ide` and both trait peeks
— is met. Close-out lands separately as `0.52.0`
(todo→done, no code). See
`notes/chores/chores-11.md > refactor: remove
is_detached_exec from trait (0.52.0-3)` for the full
record.

- Cargo.toml: 0.52.0-2 → 0.52.0-3.
- src/subcommand.rs: `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec`
  removed; `dispatch` drops the `sb_ide` call; doc
  rewritten.
- src/finalize.rs: `is_detached_exec` override on
  `FinalizeArgs` removed.
- src/main.rs: `pub fn sb_ide` deleted; main adds an
  inline `matches!(&cmd, Commands::Finalize(args) if
  args.exec)` peek and calls
  `finalize::surface_previous_failures` before
  loading Context.
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: backfilled
  `Commits: [[4]]` on the 0.52.0-2 section; new
  0.52.0-3 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.52.0-2 → (done); ladder reworded
  (close-out is the only remaining step).
```

> DEVELOPER

Actually I was asking a question, this rule is always call finalize::surface_previous_failures execpt if the current command is a Commands::Finalize command, is that correct?

> DEVELOPER

Actually I was asking a question. The rule is, always call finalize::surface_previous_failures execpt if the current command is a Commands::Finalize command, is that correct?

> AGENT

Not quite — the actual rule is finer:

- **Skip** only when: `Commands::Finalize(args) AND args.exec == true` (the detached child re-entry).
- **Surface** in every other case, including `vc-x1 finalize` *without* `--exec` (the user-initiated parent, before it spawns the detached child).

The reason for the finer rule: the user-initiated `vc-x1 finalize` is a normal interactive run from the user's POV — they should still see any failure markers from previous detached children before this run starts. Only the detached child itself (`finalize --exec`) needs to skip, because surfacing destroys the markers and the child's log isn't where the user reads them.

So the simpler "skip all Finalize" rule would lose visibility on the most common finalize entry point.

Want to keep the finer rule (current code) or accept the visibility loss for syntactic simplicity?

> DEVELOPER

So that means most (99%) of the time the surface_previous_failures is doing nothing because the previous command wasn't a finalize, just trying to understand.

> AGENT

Correct. `surface_previous_failures` does a single `read_dir` on `~/.vc-x1/finalize-fail-*` (or wherever `status_dir()` points), and if there are no `.status` entries it returns immediately. The doc on the function already notes this: "Cheap no-op when the directory doesn't exist." So in steady state (no failed background finalize), every command pays one cheap dir-stat and that's it.

The 1% where it matters: a detached `finalize --exec` child failed in the background, wrote a marker, the parent had already exited successfully — so the user has no way to know. The marker sits there until the *next* `vc-x1 <anything>` runs, surfaces it (prints to stderr), and consumes it. That's the whole point of the mechanism: failure visibility across the detach boundary.

> DEVELOPER

By the same token if the user doesn't execute another vc-x1 command for a week it'll never be found? What if multiple `vc-x1 xxx` commands are running simultaneously?

> AGENT

Both are real gaps in the current design:

**Stale markers**: yes — if the user doesn't run `vc-x1` for a week (or forever), the marker just sits on disk. The surfacing model is "next interactive run finds it." If you never run again, you never see it. Cost is acceptable for the workspace tooling case (user comes back eventually), but in a hands-off scenario (CI, scheduled jobs) the failure goes unnoticed.

**Concurrent surface calls**: also real. Two `vc-x1 X` runs in parallel would both `read_dir`, both find the same markers, both print, both attempt `remove_file`. One delete succeeds, the other gets `ENOENT` (silently swallowed by `let _ = remove_file(...)`). So the user would see the same failure printed twice. Annoying but not data-loss.

Worse case: a `finalize --exec` child is **mid-write** to a marker (e.g., still appending the error body) while a sibling `vc-x1 chid` runs and surfaces. Depending on how `write_failure_marker` writes (atomic-rename vs. direct-write), you could get truncated/garbled marker text printed. Would need to check `write_failure_marker`'s implementation to know.

Neither is critical (and both predate this cycle — out of scope for sb_ide elimination), but they're worth a `## Todo` entry if you want to tighten the model. Lock file around surface, atomic-rename on write, optional notify-at-failure for CI use, etc.

> DEVELOPER

So the code is "always" run surface_previous_failures and we'll put a test so we won't
stub our toes for one particular case, which might help but maybe not, and we'll
definitely mess up in other ways. So this is a bug, not dangerous or disastrous, but a bug.

> AGENT

Fair framing. The current gate is a partial mitigation — it covers exactly one race (detached-child-eats-its-own-prior-markers) and leaves the others (stale-forever, concurrent-double-print, mid-write torn read) on the floor. So yes, the design has a bug; the gate just hides one symptom.

Question is whether the gate earns its keep until the broader fix lands:

- **Keep it (current code)**: small, complete-feeling per-arm match; covers the one race we know is most likely (the exec child runs *after* the parent already created markers). Cost: maintains a special case forever.
- **Drop it**: code shrinks to one unconditional `surface_previous_failures()` call. Cost: detached child *will* silently consume any prior markers and the user loses that surfacing on their next interactive run. The exec-child race is the most likely one in practice (every detach cycle hits it), so we'd be trading "least-visible bug we *do* fix" for "code stays uniform."

My lean: keep the gate — it's the one race we can detect cheaply and reliably, and trading it for code uniformity costs visibility on the most-frequent failure path. File the broader race/staleness/atomicity questions as a `## Todo` so the design problem is recorded.

But it's a judgment call and "drop the gate, accept the bug, design a proper fix later" is also defensible. Which way do you want to go?

> DEVELOPER

keep the gate, add a `## Bugs` section in todo.md adding the bug. Then point to it from the code comment.

> AGENT

Adding the `## Bugs` section + bug entry + code-comment pointer.

287	   (e.g. `vc-x1 --workspace-tmp …`)? Test binaries can't
288	   easily accept arbitrary flags via `cargo test --`, so
289	   env is the realistic surface for tests; for the
290	   `vc-x1` binary itself a flag is feasible but unclear
291	   it adds value over the resolution chain.

296	and older `## Done` sections are moved to [done.md](done.md) to keep this file small.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/notes/todo.md",
  "old_string": "   env is the realistic surface for tests; for the\n   `vc-x1` binary itself a flag is feasible but unclear\n   it adds value over the resolution chain.\n\n## Done",
  "new_string": "   env is the realistic surface for tests; for the\n   `vc-x1` binary itself a flag is feasible but unclear\n   it adds value over the resolution chain.\n\n## Bugs\n\nKnown defects we're aware of but haven't scheduled a fix\nfor. Each entry should describe what goes wrong, when,\nand the cost of the failure. Same lazy `1.` numbering as\n`## Todo` — markdown auto-numbers, reorder/insert without\nrenumbering.\n\n1. **`finalize::surface_previous_failures` is racy and\n   bounded by \"next vc-x1 run\".** The current model has\n   four gaps:\n   - **Stale forever.** Markers sit on disk until the\n     next `vc-x1 <anything>` runs. If the user abandons\n     the workspace (e.g., CI / scheduled use), failures\n     are never surfaced.\n   - **Concurrent surface_previous_failures.** Two\n     `vc-x1` runs racing: both `read_dir`, both print\n     the marker, one deletes (the other's `remove_file`\n     silently fails). The user sees the same failure\n     printed twice.\n   - **Mid-write torn read.** A `finalize --exec` child\n     writing a marker while a sibling `vc-x1` is\n     surfacing could read partial content. Atomic-rename\n     on write would close this.\n   - **No notify-at-failure path.** A detached\n     `finalize --exec` failure only becomes visible when\n     the user next runs *any* `vc-x1` command — fine for\n     interactive use, invisible for CI / scheduled use\n     where there may be no next run.\n\n   The exec-child gate in `main.rs` (since 0.52.0-3)\n   patches one related case — the detached child eating\n   its own prior markers before the user can see them —\n   but doesn't address any of the above. Holistic fix\n   needs locking + atomic writes + maybe a\n   notify-at-failure path for hands-off use.\n\n## Done"
}
```

> AGENT

Revised commit body (match form + bugs section):

```
refactor: remove is_detached_exec from trait (0.52.0-3)
```

```
Third sb_ide-elimination substep. The
`SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec` trait method goes
away, taking `sb_ide` with it. The detached-exec gate
is finalize-specific machinery —
`finalize::surface_previous_failures` is the only
behavior it ever protected, and `FinalizeArgs.exec`
is the only field that ever set it. Lifting the gate
into `main` (one `match` arm against
`Commands::Finalize`) cuts the trait peek + its sole
override + the free function that consumed it.

After this substep the `SubcommandRunner` trait
surface is the load-bearing minimum (`to_params`,
`run`, `dispatch`); `main` owns the
surface-previous-failures call with the exec-child
skip inline at the call site; `dispatch` has no
session-chrome responsibility at all. The cycle's
stated goal — eliminate `sb_ide` and both trait peeks
— is met. Close-out lands separately as `0.52.0`
(todo→done, no code).

While we were here: the exec-child gate covers one
surfacing race; the broader model has stale-forever,
concurrent-double-print, mid-write-torn-read, and
no-notify gaps. Captured as a new `## Bugs` section
in `notes/todo.md` and referenced from the inline
comment.

See `notes/chores/chores-11.md > refactor: remove
is_detached_exec from trait (0.52.0-3)` for the full
record.

- Cargo.toml: 0.52.0-2 → 0.52.0-3.
- src/subcommand.rs: `SubcommandRunner::is_detached_exec`
  removed; `dispatch` drops the `sb_ide` call; doc
  rewritten.
- src/finalize.rs: `is_detached_exec` override on
  `FinalizeArgs` removed.
- src/main.rs: `pub fn sb_ide` deleted; main adds an
  inline `match` against `Commands::Finalize(args)`
  calling `finalize::surface_previous_failures`
  unless `args.exec`; comment points at the Bugs
  entry.
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: backfilled
  `Commits: [[4]]` on the 0.52.0-2 section; new
  0.52.0-3 section.
- notes/todo.md: 0.52.0-2 → (done); ladder reworded;
  new `## Bugs` section with the
  `surface_previous_failures` design gaps.
```

OK to push?

chore: close sb_ide elimination (0.52.0)

```
Cycle close-out. All three pieces of per-run "session
chrome" that hung off `SubcommandRunner` after the
0.50.0 trait sweep are gone: `sb_ide` (free function)
plus the trait peek methods `suppress_banner` and
`is_detached_exec`. After this cycle the trait surface
is the load-bearing minimum — `to_params`, `run`, and
the default `dispatch` (which builds params, brackets
`bm_track` enter/exit, runs, maps exit code). No peeks,
no chrome.

User-visible changes the cycle landed:

- Banner off by default; `-V` opts in and prints the
  banner as the first line while the subcommand still
  runs (scripts can capture version + output in one
  go).
- `bm_track` emits at `debug!` — default runs stay
  quiet, signal stays available under `-v`.
- `surface_previous_failures` still fires before every
  command (except the detached `finalize --exec`
  child); broader surfacing-race gaps captured as a
  new `## Bugs` section in `notes/todo.md`.

See `notes/chores/chores-11.md > chore: close sb_ide
elimination (0.52.0)` for the as-built ladder + outcome
notes.

- Cargo.toml: 0.52.0-3 → 0.52.0 (suffix dropped).
- notes/chores/chores-11.md: backfilled
  `Commits: [[5]]` on 0.52.0-3; new close-out
  section.
- notes/todo.md: In Progress ladder deleted; added
  `sb_ide elimination — banner off by default (-V
  toggles), bm_track → debug!, sb_ide +
  SubcommandRunner::{is_detached_exec,
  suppress_banner} removed (0.52.0)` to `## Done`
  with `[[2]]` ref.
```
